index.php
```
<?php
// 1. Initialize user session tracking
session_start();

// 2. Route Protection: Redirect to login view if user credentials are uninitialized
if (!isset($_SESSION['logged_in']) || $_SESSION['logged_in'] !== true || !isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header("Location: ../../authentication/login.php");
    exit;
}

// 3. Connect to database module and load business service
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/db.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../api/DashboardService.php';

$userId = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

// --- DATA ORCHESTRATION ---
$profileData    = DashboardService::getUserProfile($conn, $userId);
$metricsData    = DashboardService::getMetrics($conn, $userId);
$streakData     = DashboardService::getStreakAndHeatmapData($conn, $userId);
$recentSessions = DashboardService::getRecentSessions($conn, $userId, 4);

// Extracted variables for template includes
$displayName    = $profileData['displayName'];
$current_streak  = $streakData['currentStreak'];
$pageTitle      = "Home Dashboard";
$currentPage    = 'home';

/**
 * Helper function for Heatmap grid CSS class matching review intensity.
 */
if (!function_exists('heatmap_level_class')) {
    function heatmap_level_class(int $reviews): string {
        if ($reviews <= 0) return 'bg-surface-container-high';
        if ($reviews <= 14) return 'bg-primary-fixed';
        if ($reviews <= 39) return 'bg-primary-fixed-dim';
        if ($reviews <= 79) return 'bg-primary-container';
        return 'bg-primary';
    }
}
?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">

<?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/head.php'; ?>

<!-- BODY: Main App Background & Styling Root -->
<body class="bg-background font-body-md text-on-surface antialiased min-h-screen selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-on-primary-fixed">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <!-- MAIN: Viewport Shell Offset for Mobile/Desktop Navigation Bar -->
    <main class="pt-16 pl-0 md:pl-72 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out">
        <?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/header-data.php'; ?>    
        <?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/header.php'; ?>
        <?php include __DIR__ . '/dashboard.php'; ?>                
    </main>

    <!-- 1. DATA INJECTION: Server-side PHP metrics passed safely to client-side JS -->
    <script>
        window.MemPulseHomeData = {
            stats: {
                cardsDue: <?php echo (int) $metricsData['cardsDue']; ?>,
                masteredCards: <?php echo (int) $metricsData['masteredCards']; ?>,
                toughCards: <?php echo (int) $metricsData['toughCards']; ?>,
                currentStreak: <?php echo (int) $streakData['currentStreak']; ?>,
                bestStreak: <?php echo (int) $streakData['bestStreak']; ?>,
                streakAccuracy: <?php echo (float) $streakData['streakAccuracy']; ?>
            }
        };

        /**
         * Global Dashboard UI Manager
         * Synchronizes dynamic metrics across header, sidebar, and metric panels.
         */
        const MemPulseDashboard = {
            updateAll(payload) {
                if (!payload) return;
                this.syncHeader(payload.user);
                this.syncSidebarProfile(payload.user);
                this.syncMetrics(payload.stats);
            },

            syncMetrics(stats) {
                if (!stats) return;
                this.setElText('stat-cards-due', stats.cardsDue);
                this.setElText('stat-mastered', stats.masteredCards);
                this.setElText('stat-tough', stats.toughCards);
                this.setElText('stat-current-streak', stats.currentStreak);
                this.setElText('stat-best-streak', stats.bestStreak);
                this.setElText('stat-streak-accuracy', stats.streakAccuracy);
            },

            setElText(id, value) {
                const el = document.getElementById(id);
                if (el) el.textContent = value ?? '--';
            }
        };

        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
            MemPulseDashboard.updateAll(window.MemPulseHomeData);

            const heatmapScroller = document.getElementById("heatmap-scroll");
            if (heatmapScroller) {
                requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                    heatmapScroller.scrollLeft = heatmapScroller.scrollWidth - heatmapScroller.clientWidth;
                });
            }
        });
    </script>

    <!-- 2. CLIENT-SIDE ASSET IMPORTS: Separated standalone JavaScript modules -->
    <script src="../assets/js/hero-carousel.js" defer></script>
</body>
</html>
```

```
                $dueSubtitle = 'Keep your ' . $curStreak . '-day streak alive';
            } elseif (count($dueDecks) === 1) {
                $dueSubtitle = 'in ' . $dueDecks[0]['title'];
            } elseif (count($dueDecks) > 1) {
                $dueSubtitle = 'across ' . count($dueDecks) . ' decks';
            } else {
                $dueSubtitle = '';
            }

            if ($nextDueSecs !== null && $nextDueSecs > 0) {

                elseif ($nextDueSecs < 86400)  $nextDueLabel = 'Next cards due in ' . (int) floor($nextDueSecs / 3600) . ' h';
                else                           $nextDueLabel = 'Next cards due in ' . (int) floor($nextDueSecs / 86400) . ' d';
            }

                        <?php
                        $weekStart = clone $streakData['calendarStart'];
                        for ($w = 0; $w < 53; $w++) {
                            for ($d = 0; $d < 7; $d++) {
                                $date = clone $weekStart;
                                $date->modify('+' . $d . ' days');

                                if ($date < $streakData['heatmapStart'] || $date > $streakData['heatmapEnd']) continue;

                                if ($date->format('j') === '1' && $w <= 51) {
                                    echo '<div class="text-[10px] leading-none font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-on-surface-variant" style="grid-row: 1; grid-column: ' . ($w + 2) . ' / span 3;">' . htmlspecialchars($date->format('M'), ENT_QUOTES) . '</div>';
                                }

                                $key = $date->format('Y-m-d');
                                $reviews = $streakData['heatmapData'][$key] ?? 0;
                                $tooltip = $date->format('M j, Y') . ' • ' . ($reviews === 0 ? 'No reviews' : ($reviews === 1 ? '1 review' : $reviews . ' reviews'));
                                echo '<div class="heatmap-cell rounded-[4px] transition-transform hover:scale-110 ' . heatmap_level_class($reviews) . '" style="grid-row: ' . ($d + 2) . '; grid-column: ' . ($w + 2) . ';" data-tooltip="' . htmlspecialchars($tooltip, ENT_QUOTES) . '"></div>';
                            }
                            $weekStart->modify('+7 days');
                        }
                        ?>
                    </div>
                </div>
```

deck-view.php
```
<?php
/**
 * Deck View Template with Tabulator Data Grid & Persistent Sidebar Card Editor / Mobile Modal
 */
?>
<link href="https://unpkg.com/tabulator-tables@5.5.0/dist/css/tabulator_simple.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
<script type="text/javascript" src="https://unpkg.com/tabulator-tables@5.5.0/dist/js/tabulator.min.js"></script>

<script>
    window.MemPulseDeckData = window.MemPulseDeckData || {};
    window.MemPulseDeckData.flashcards = <?= json_encode(array_values($flashcards ?? []), JSON_HEX_TAG | JSON_HEX_AMP | JSON_HEX_APOS | JSON_HEX_QUOT); ?>;

</script>
```

```
            <?php 
            $bannerDir = __DIR__ . '/../assets/images/deck-header-banner/';
            $bannerFiles = glob($bannerDir . '*.{jpg,jpeg,png,webp,svg}', GLOB_BRACE);
            
            // Sort files numerically (e.g., dh1, dh2, dh10)
            natsort($bannerFiles);

            foreach ($bannerFiles as $filePath): 
                $fileName = basename($filePath);
                $relativePath = '../assets/images/deck-header-banner/' . $fileName;
            ?>
                <button type="button" data-banner-path="<?= DeckService::h($relativePath) ?>" class="banner-option border-2 border-transparent hover:border-primary rounded-xl h-20 overflow-hidden relative transition-all group">
                    <img src="<?= DeckService::h($relativePath) ?>" class="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-200" alt="<?= DeckService::h($fileName) ?>">
                </button>
```

deck.php
```
<?php
// 1. Initialize user session tracking
session_start();

// 2. Route Protection: Redirect to login view if user credentials are uninitialized
if (!isset($_SESSION['logged_in']) || $_SESSION['logged_in'] !== true || !isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header("Location: ../../authentication/login.php");
    exit;
}

// 3. Connect to database module and load business services
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/db.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../api/FlashcardService.php';

$userId = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

ArchiveService::purgeExpired($conn, $userId);

// 4. Validate and sanitize input deck ID parameter
$deckId = isset($_GET['deck_id']) && ctype_digit((string) $_GET['deck_id']) ? (int) $_GET['deck_id'] : 0;

// 5. Fetch target deck details and verify user ownership
$deck = FlashcardService::getDeckDetails($conn, $deckId, $userId);

// Redirect to deck list if the requested deck is missing or unauthorized
if (!$deck) {
    header("Location: decks.php");
    exit;
}

// 6. Fetch all active flashcards belonging to the deck
$flashcards = FlashcardService::getDeckFlashcards($conn, $deckId);
$archivedFlashcards = FlashcardService::getDeckFlashcards($conn, $deckId, '', true);

// 6.5 Fetch user's active decks for the Move dialog
$userDecks = FlashcardService::getUserDecks($conn, $userId);
$otherDecks = array_filter($userDecks, fn($d) => $d['id'] !== $deckId);

// 7. CSRF token initialization for card mutation requests (Add / Edit / Delete)
if (empty($_SESSION['csrf_token'])) {
    $_SESSION['csrf_token'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
}

$pageTitle   = $deck['title'] . " - Flashcards";
$currentPage = "decks";
?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">

<?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/head.php'; ?>

<!-- BODY: Main App Background & Styling Root -->
<body class="bg-background font-body-md text-on-surface antialiased min-h-screen selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-on-primary-fixed">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <!-- MAIN: Viewport Shell Offset for Mobile/Desktop Navigation Bar -->
    <main class="pl-0 md:pl-72 pt-16 md:pt-12 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out">
        <?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/header-data.php'; ?>
        <?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/header.php'; ?>
        <?php include __DIR__ . '/deck-view.php'; ?>
    </main>

    <!-- 1. DATA INJECTION: Server-side PHP variables passed safely to client-side JS -->
    <script>
        window.MemPulseDeckData = Object.assign(window.MemPulseDeckData || {}, {
            csrfToken: <?= json_encode($_SESSION['csrf_token'] ?? '') ?>,
            deckId: <?= $deckId ?>,
            cardCount: <?= (int) $deck['card_count'] ?>,
            archivedCount: <?= (int) $deck['archived_count'] ?>,
            retentionDays: <?= ArchiveService::RETENTION_DAYS ?>,
            otherDecks: <?= json_encode(array_values($otherDecks)) ?>
        });
    </script>

    <!-- 2. CLIENT-SIDE ASSET IMPORTS: Separated standalone JavaScript module -->
    <script src="../assets/js/archive-ui.js" defer></script>
    <script src="../assets/js/deck.js" defer></script>
</body>
</html>
```

```
<?php
// 1. Initialize user session tracking
session_start();

// 2. Route Protection: Redirect to login view if user credentials are uninitialized
if (!isset($_SESSION['logged_in']) || $_SESSION['logged_in'] !== true || !isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header("Location: ../../authentication/login.php");
    exit;
}

// 3. Connect to database module and load business service
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/db.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../api/DeckService.php';

// CSRF token used by the rename/archive/create/restore/add_card requests
if (empty($_SESSION['csrf_token'])) {
    $_SESSION['csrf_token'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
}

$userId = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

// --- DATA ORCHESTRATION ---
$decks         = DeckService::getUserDecks($conn, $userId);
$archivedDecks = DeckService::getArchivedDecks($conn, $userId);
$deckTotal     = count($decks);

$pageTitle   = "Decks";
$currentPage = "decks";
?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">

<?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/head.php'; ?>

<!-- BODY: Main App Background & Styling Root -->
<body class="bg-background font-body-md text-on-surface antialiased min-h-screen selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-on-primary-fixed">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <!-- MAIN: Viewport Shell Offset for Mobile/Desktop Navigation Bar -->
    <main class="pl-0 md:pl-72 pt-16 md:pt-12 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out">
        <?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/header-data.php'; ?>
        <?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/header.php'; ?>
        <?php include __DIR__ . '/decks-view.php'; ?>
    </main>

    <!-- 1. DATA INJECTION: Server-side PHP variables passed safely to client-side JS -->
    <script>
        window.MemPulseDeckData = {
            csrfToken: <?= json_encode($_SESSION['csrf_token'] ?? '') ?>,
            createRequested: <?= isset($_GET['create']) && $_GET['create'] === '1' ? 'true' : 'false' ?>,
            addCardRequested: <?= isset($_GET['add_card']) && $_GET['add_card'] === '1' ? 'true' : 'false' ?>,
            requestedDeckId: <?= isset($_GET['deck_id']) && ctype_digit((string) $_GET['deck_id']) ? (int) $_GET['deck_id'] : 'null' ?>
        };
    </script>

    <!-- 2. CLIENT-SIDE ASSET IMPORTS: Separated standalone JavaScript modules -->
    <script src="../assets/js/decks.js" defer></script>
</body>
</html>
```

review-hub-view.php
```
<?php
/**
 * Review Hub view (STATE A of review.php)
 *
 * Expects these variables from review.php:
 *   $dueDecks, $upcomingDecks, $recentSessions  (see ReviewHubService)
 *   $dueTotal, $nextDueSecs, $curStreak, $reviewedToday
 */

$h = [ReviewHubService::class, 'h'];

// ---- Due Now call-to-action state ----
$dueDeckCount = count($dueDecks);
$reviewHref   = $dueDeckCount > 0 ? 'review.php?deck_id=' . (int) $dueDecks[0]['id'] : 'review.php';
$streakAtRisk = $dueTotal > 0 && $curStreak > 0 && $reviewedToday === 0;

if ($streakAtRisk) {
    $dueSubtitle = 'Keep your ' . $curStreak . '-day streak alive';
} elseif ($dueDeckCount === 1) {
    $dueSubtitle = 'in ' . $dueDecks[0]['title'];
} elseif ($dueDeckCount > 1) {
    $dueSubtitle = 'Starting with ' . $dueDecks[0]['title'] . ' (+' . ($dueDeckCount - 1) . ' more)';
} else {
    $dueSubtitle = '';
}

$nextDueLabel = ($nextDueSecs !== null && $nextDueSecs > 0)
    ? 'Next cards due in ' . ReviewHubService::formatSpan($nextDueSecs)
    : '';
?>
<!-- ========================================================================= -->
<!-- STATE A: REVIEW HUB (dashboard-style layout, table lists)                 -->
<!-- ========================================================================= -->
<div class="flex flex-col w-full gap-4 sm:gap-6">

    <!-- DIV: ROW 1 - Due Now call-to-action (left) vs Keyboard controls (right) -->
    <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-4 sm:gap-6 items-stretch w-full">

        <!-- DIV: LEFT - Due Now call-to-action -->
        <div class="lg:col-span-6 flex w-full min-w-0">

            <?php if ($dueTotal > 0): ?>
                <!-- A: Cards due - primary call-to-action -->
                <a href="<?= $h($reviewHref) ?>"
                   id="stat-due-cta"
                   aria-label="Review <?= (int) $dueTotal ?> due <?= $dueTotal === 1 ? 'card' : 'cards' ?>"
                   class="group/cta relative overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-between gap-4 w-full h-full min-h-[84px] px-4 sm:px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-primary to-primary-container text-on-primary shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:-translate-y-0.5 transition-all">
                    <div class="absolute -right-6 -top-8 w-28 h-28 bg-white/15 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none"></div>

                    <div class="relative z-10 flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                        <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                            <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">alarm</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex flex-col min-w-0">
                            <span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-on-primary/80">Due Now</span>
                            <span class="text-base sm:text-lg font-extrabold leading-tight truncate">
                                <span id="stat-due-cta-count"><?= (int) $dueTotal ?></span>
                                <?= $dueTotal === 1 ? 'card' : 'cards' ?> ready to review
                            </span>
                            <?php if ($dueSubtitle !== ''): ?>
                                <span class="text-[11px] sm:text-xs text-on-primary/80 truncate"><?= $h($dueSubtitle) ?></span>
                            <?php endif; ?>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <span class="relative z-10 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 sm:px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white text-primary font-label-md text-xs font-bold shadow-xs flex-shrink-0">
                        <span class="hidden sm:inline">Start Review</span>
                        <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] transition-transform group-hover/cta:translate-x-1">arrow_forward</span>
                    </span>
                </a>

            <?php elseif ($nextDueSecs !== null): ?>
                <!-- B: Nothing due, cards are scheduled -->
                <div id="stat-due-cta" class="flex items-center gap-3 w-full h-full min-h-[84px] px-4 sm:px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/30 shadow-xs">
                    <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200/80 flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-600 flex-shrink-0">
                        <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">task_alt</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex flex-col min-w-0">
                        <span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant/70">Due Now</span>
                        <span class="text-base sm:text-lg font-extrabold leading-tight text-on-surface">All caught up</span>
                        <?php if ($nextDueLabel !== ''): ?>
                            <span class="text-[11px] sm:text-xs text-on-surface-variant"><?= $h($nextDueLabel) ?></span>
                        <?php endif; ?>
                    </div>
                </div>

            <?php else: ?>
                <!-- C: No scheduled cards at all - point to creation -->
                <a href="decks.php?add_card=1" id="stat-due-cta"
```

review.php

```
<?php
session_start();

if (!isset($_SESSION['logged_in']) || $_SESSION['logged_in'] !== true || !isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header("Location: ../authentication/login.php");
    exit;
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/db.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../api/ReviewHubService.php';

// Anti-CSRF token, sent along with the batch save in review.js
if (empty($_SESSION['csrf_token'])) {
    $_SESSION['csrf_token'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
}

$userId = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$deckId = isset($_GET['deck_id']) && ctype_digit((string) $_GET['deck_id']) ? (int) $_GET['deck_id'] : null;

$isReviewing = false;
$dueCards = [];
$hubDecks = [];

if ($deckId) {
    // ACTIVE REVIEW STATE: Fetch due cards for the specific deck
    $isReviewing = true;
    // Ownership check: only this user's non-archived decks, non-archived cards
    $sql = "SELECT f.id, f.front, f.back, f.interval_days, f.next_review_at
            FROM flashcards f
            JOIN decks d ON d.id = f.deck_id
            WHERE f.deck_id = ? AND d.user_id = ? AND d.deleted_at IS NULL
              AND f.deleted_at IS NULL AND f.next_review_at <= NOW()
            ORDER BY f.next_review_at ASC";
    if ($stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql)) {
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ii", $deckId, $userId);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);
        while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
            $dueCards[] = $row;
        }
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
    }
} else {
    // HUB STATE: table data + call-to-action data
    $hubDecks       = ReviewHubService::getHubDecks($conn, $userId);
    $dueDecks       = ReviewHubService::dueDecks($hubDecks);
    $upcomingDecks  = ReviewHubService::upcomingDecks($hubDecks);
    $recentSessions = ReviewHubService::getRecentSessions($conn, $userId, 8);

    $streakInfo     = ReviewHubService::getStreakInfo($conn, $userId);
    $curStreak      = $streakInfo['currentStreak'];
    $reviewedToday  = $streakInfo['reviewedToday'];

    $dueTotal       = array_sum(array_column($dueDecks, 'dueCount'));
    $nextDueSecs    = $dueTotal === 0 ? ReviewHubService::getNextDueSeconds($conn, $userId) : null;
}

$pageTitle = $isReviewing ? "Active Review" : "Review Hub"; 
$currentPage = "review";
?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">

<?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/head.php'; ?>

<body class="bg-background font-body-md text-on-surface antialiased min-h-screen">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <main class="pt-16 pl-0 md:pl-72 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out h-screen flex flex-col">
        <?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/header-data.php'; ?>    
        <?php include __DIR__ . '/../includes/header.php'; ?>
        
        <div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-6">
            
            <?php if (!$isReviewing): ?>
                <?php include __DIR__ . '/review-hub-view.php'; ?>

            <?php else: ?>
                <!-- STATE B: ACTIVE REVIEW INTERFACE -->
                <div id="active-review-container" class="max-w-3xl mx-auto flex flex-col h-full <?= empty($dueCards) ? 'hidden' : '' ?>">
                    
                    <!-- Progress & Tally Header -->
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-4 flex-1">
                            <button id="end-session-btn" class="text-on-surface-variant hover:text-rose-600 transition-colors" title="End Session Early">
                                <span class="material-symbols-outlined">close</span>
                            </button>
                            <div class="flex-1 max-w-xs">
                                <div class="flex justify-between text-xs font-semibold mb-1">
                                    <span id="progress-text" class="text-on-surface-variant">Card 1 of <?= count($dueCards) ?></span>
                                </div>
                                <div class="w-full bg-surface-container-high rounded-full h-1.5 overflow-hidden">
                                    <div id="progress-bar" class="bg-primary h-1.5 rounded-full" style="width: 0%"></div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        
                        <!-- Real-time Tally Counters -->
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm font-bold bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/30 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl shadow-xs">
                            <span class="text-emerald-600 flex items-center gap-1" title="Remembered">
                                <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">check_circle</span>
                                <span id="tally-remembered">0</span>
                            </span>
                            <span class="text-outline-variant">|</span>
                            <span class="text-rose-600 flex items-center gap-1" title="Forgotten">
                                <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">cancel</span>
                                <span id="tally-forgotten">0</span>
                            </span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- Flashcard Stage -->
                    <div class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[350px]">
                        <div id="flashcard-stage" class="w-full bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/30 shadow-md rounded-2xl p-8 sm:p-12 text-center relative overflow-hidden transition-all">
                            
                            <!-- Rating Badge (shows briefly when navigating backwards) -->
                            <div id="historical-rating-badge" class="hidden absolute top-4 right-4 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider"></div>

                            <!-- Front -->

                            <div id="card-back-content" class="text-lg sm:text-xl text-on-surface-variant hidden"></div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- Controls Action Bar -->
                    <div class="mt-8 flex justify-center min-h-[60px]">
                        <!-- State 1: Show Answer -->
                        <button id="show-answer-btn" class="px-8 py-3 rounded-xl bg-primary text-on-primary font-bold shadow-md hover:bg-primary-container hover:-translate-y-0.5 transition-all w-full max-w-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                            <span>Show Answer</span>
                            <kbd class="hidden sm:inline-block px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-mono bg-white/20 rounded">Space</kbd>
                        </button>

                        <!-- State 2: Rate Actions (Hidden initially) -->
                        <div id="rate-actions" class="hidden flex gap-4 w-full max-w-md">
                            <button id="rate-forgot-btn" class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-100 transition-colors shadow-sm relative group">
                                <span class="font-bold">Forgot</span>
                                <span id="projected-forgot-date" class="text-[11px] font-medium opacity-80 mt-0.5">Due: Today</span>
                                <kbd class="absolute -top-2 -right-2 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-mono bg-surface border border-outline-variant rounded text-on-surface-variant opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">F</kbd>
                            </button>
                            <button id="rate-remember-btn" class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-100 transition-colors shadow-sm relative group">
                                <span class="font-bold">Remember</span>
                                <span id="projected-remember-date" class="text-[11px] font-medium opacity-80 mt-0.5">Due: Next</span>
                                <kbd class="absolute -top-2 -right-2 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-mono bg-surface border border-outline-variant rounded text-on-surface-variant opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">Space</kbd>
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                </div>

                <!-- STATE C: ZERO CARDS / FINISHED SCREEN -->
                <div id="review-finished-screen" class="max-w-2xl mx-auto flex-col items-center justify-center text-center h-full pt-12 <?= empty($dueCards) ? 'flex' : 'hidden' ?>">
                    <div class="w-20 h-20 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-6">
                        <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[40px]">task_alt</span>
                    </div>
                    <h2 class="font-headline-md text-3xl font-extrabold text-on-surface mb-2">Session Complete!</h2>
```

```
<?php
if (!defined('BASE_URL')) {
    define('BASE_URL', '/mempulse/app/');
}
?>
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
```

header-data.php
```
<?php
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

// Ensure database connection is present
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/db.php';

$user_id = $_SESSION['user_id'] ?? null;

// Only fetch if not already populated by page controller
if (!isset($displayName)) {
    $displayName = 'Active Learner';
    if ($user_id) {
        $user_sql = "SELECT first_name, last_name FROM users WHERE id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL";
        if ($user_stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $user_sql)) {
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($user_stmt, "i", $user_id);
            mysqli_stmt_execute($user_stmt);
            mysqli_stmt_bind_result($user_stmt, $first_name, $last_name);
            mysqli_stmt_fetch($user_stmt);
            mysqli_stmt_close($user_stmt);

            $fullName = trim(($first_name ?? '') . ' ' . ($last_name ?? ''));
            if ($fullName !== '') {
                $displayName = $fullName;
            }
        }
    }
}

if (!isset($current_streak)) {
    $current_streak = 0;
    if ($user_id) {
        $session_sql = "SELECT DATE(started_at) AS session_date
                        FROM review_sessions
                        WHERE user_id = ?
                          AND ended_at IS NOT NULL
                          AND total_reviewed > 0
                        GROUP BY DATE(started_at)
                        ORDER BY session_date DESC";

        if ($session_stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $session_sql)) {
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($session_stmt, "i", $user_id);
            mysqli_stmt_execute($session_stmt);
            $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($session_stmt);

            $activity_dates = [];
            while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
                $activity_dates[] = $row['session_date'];
            }
            mysqli_stmt_close($session_stmt);

            if (!empty($activity_dates)) {
                $today = date('Y-m-d');
                $yesterday = date('Y-m-d', strtotime('-1 day'));

                if ($activity_dates[0] === $today || $activity_dates[0] === $yesterday) {
                    $check_date = new DateTime($activity_dates[0]);
                    foreach ($activity_dates as $date) {
                        if ($date === $check_date->format('Y-m-d')) {
                            $current_streak++;
                            $check_date->modify('-1 day');
                        } else {
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```
<?php
if (!defined('BASE_URL')) {
    define('BASE_URL', '/mempulse/app/');
}
$currentPage = $currentPage ?? '';

// Calculate total due cards globally for the sidebar badge
$sidebarDueCount = 0;
if (isset($_SESSION['user_id']) && isset($conn)) {
    $sidebarSql = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM flashcards f 
                   JOIN decks d ON f.deck_id = d.id 
                   WHERE d.user_id = ? AND f.deleted_at IS NULL AND f.next_review_at <= NOW()";
    if ($sidebarStmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sidebarSql)) {
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($sidebarStmt, "i", $_SESSION['user_id']);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($sidebarStmt);
        mysqli_stmt_bind_result($sidebarStmt, $sidebarDueCount);
        mysqli_stmt_fetch($sidebarStmt);
        mysqli_stmt_close($sidebarStmt);

<script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
        const sidebar = document.querySelector("#sidebar");
        const mainContent = document.querySelector("main");
        const toggleBtn = document.querySelector("#sidebar-toggle");
        const mobileToggleBtn = document.querySelector("#mobile-menu-toggle");
        const mobileOverlay = document.querySelector("#mobile-sidebar-overlay");

        if (!sidebar) return;

        const toggleIcon = toggleBtn ? toggleBtn.querySelector(".material-symbols-outlined") : null;

        const sidebarCollapsed = localStorage.getItem("sidebarCollapsed") === "true";
        if (sidebarCollapsed && window.innerWidth >= 768) {
            sidebar.classList.add("collapsed");
            if (mainContent) {
                mainContent.classList.add("collapsed");
            }
            if (toggleIcon) {
                toggleIcon.textContent = "left_panel_open";
            }
        }

        if (toggleBtn) {
            toggleBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
                if (window.innerWidth < 768) {
                    closeMobileSidebar();
                    return;
                }
                
                const isCollapsed = sidebar.classList.toggle("collapsed");
                if (mainContent) {
                    mainContent.classList.toggle("collapsed", isCollapsed);
                }
                if (toggleIcon) {
                    toggleIcon.textContent = isCollapsed ? "left_panel_open" : "left_panel_close";
                }
                localStorage.setItem("sidebarCollapsed", isCollapsed);
            });
        }

        function openMobileSidebar() {
            sidebar.classList.remove("-translate-x-full");
            sidebar.classList.remove("collapsed"); 
            mobileOverlay.classList.remove("hidden");
            
            if (toggleIcon) toggleIcon.textContent = "close";
            setTimeout(() => mobileOverlay.classList.remove("opacity-0"), 10);
        }

        function closeMobileSidebar() {
            sidebar.classList.add("-translate-x-full");
            mobileOverlay.classList.add("opacity-0");

            if (toggleIcon) {
                toggleIcon.textContent = sidebar.classList.contains("collapsed") ? "left_panel_open" : "left_panel_close";
            }
            setTimeout(() => mobileOverlay.classList.add("hidden"), 300);
        }

        if (mobileToggleBtn) {
            mobileToggleBtn.addEventListener("click", openMobileSidebar);
        }
        
        if (mobileOverlay) {
            mobileOverlay.addEventListener("click", closeMobileSidebar);
        }

        const logoutBtn = document.getElementById('logout-btn');
        const logoutDialog = document.getElementById('logout-dialog');
        const logoutCancel = document.getElementById('logout-cancel');

        if (logoutBtn && logoutDialog) {
            logoutBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                logoutDialog.showModal();
            });

            if (logoutCancel) {
                logoutCancel.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    logoutDialog.close();
                });
            }

            logoutDialog.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                const dialogDimensions = logoutDialog.getBoundingClientRect();
                if (
                    e.clientX < dialogDimensions.left ||
                    e.clientX > dialogDimensions.right ||
                    e.clientY < dialogDimensions.top ||
                    e.clientY > dialogDimensions.bottom
                ) {
                    logoutDialog.close();
                }
            });
        }
    });
</script>
```

review.js
```
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    const data = window.MemPulseReviewData;
    if (!data.deckId || !data.dueCards || data.dueCards.length === 0) return;

    let queue = data.dueCards;
    let currentIndex = 0;

    // State storage: track how the user rated each card ID
    // Format: { cardId: 'remembered' | 'forgotten' }
    let ratings = {};
    let isAnswerShown = false;
    let sessionEnded = false;

    const csrfToken = data.csrfToken || '';
    const sessionStartedAt = Date.now();
    let sessionDuration = 0;
    let unsavedRatings = false;

    // DOM Elements
    const elFront = document.getElementById('card-front-content');
    const elBack = document.getElementById('card-back-content');
    const elDivider = document.getElementById('card-divider');
    const btnShow = document.getElementById('show-answer-btn');
    const boxRates = document.getElementById('rate-actions');
    const btnForgot = document.getElementById('rate-forgot-btn');
    const btnRemember = document.getElementById('rate-remember-btn');
    const projForgot = document.getElementById('projected-forgot-date');
    const projRemember = document.getElementById('projected-remember-date');

    const txtProgress = document.getElementById('progress-text');
    const barProgress = document.getElementById('progress-bar');
    const txtRem = document.getElementById('tally-remembered');
    const txtFor = document.getElementById('tally-forgotten');
    const badgeHist = document.getElementById('historical-rating-badge');

    const viewActive = document.getElementById('active-review-container');
    const viewFinished = document.getElementById('review-finished-screen');

    // 1. Core Navigation & Display Logic
    function renderCard() {
        if (currentIndex >= queue.length) {
            endSession();
            return;
        }

        const card = queue[currentIndex];
        elFront.innerHTML = escapeHtml(card.front).replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
        elBack.innerHTML = escapeHtml(card.back).replace(/\n/g, '<br>');

        txtProgress.textContent = `Card ${currentIndex + 1} of ${queue.length}`;
        barProgress.style.width = `${((currentIndex) / queue.length) * 100}%`;

        // Check if previously rated (via back navigation)
        const prevRating = ratings[card.id];
        if (prevRating) {
            badgeHist.classList.remove('hidden', 'bg-emerald-100', 'text-emerald-700', 'bg-rose-100', 'text-rose-700');
            if (prevRating === 'remembered') {
                badgeHist.textContent = 'Rated: Remembered';
                badgeHist.classList.add('bg-emerald-100', 'text-emerald-700');
            } else {
                badgeHist.textContent = 'Rated: Forgotten';
                badgeHist.classList.add('bg-rose-100', 'text-rose-700');
            }
        } else {
            badgeHist.classList.add('hidden');
        }

        // Calculate and show projected dates
        const interval = parseInt(card.interval_days) || 1;
        projForgot.textContent = "Due: Today";
        const remDays = interval === 0 ? 1 : interval * 2;
        projRemember.textContent = `Due: ${remDays} ${remDays === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'}`;

        hideAnswer();
    }

    function showAnswer() {
        if (sessionEnded) return;
        isAnswerShown = true;
        elBack.classList.remove('hidden');
        elDivider.classList.remove('hidden');
        btnShow.classList.add('hidden');
        boxRates.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function hideAnswer() {
        isAnswerShown = false;
        elBack.classList.add('hidden');
        elDivider.classList.add('hidden');
        btnShow.classList.remove('hidden');
        boxRates.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    // 2. Rating & Tally Logic
    function rateCard(status) {
        const card = queue[currentIndex];
        ratings[card.id] = status;
        unsavedRatings = true;

        updateTally();
        showFeedbackToast(status);

        currentIndex++;
        renderCard();
    }

    function updateTally() {
        let rem = 0, forg = 0;
        Object.values(ratings).forEach(r => r === 'remembered' ? rem++ : forg++);
        txtRem.textContent = rem;
        txtFor.textContent = forg;
    }

    function showFeedbackToast(status) {
        const toast = document.getElementById('review-toast');
        const icon = document.getElementById('review-toast-icon');
        const msg = document.getElementById('review-toast-msg');

        toast.classList.remove('translate-y-12', 'opacity-0', 'bg-emerald-600', 'text-white', 'bg-rose-600');
        if (status === 'remembered') {
            toast.classList.add('bg-emerald-600', 'text-white');
            icon.textContent = 'check_circle';
            msg.textContent = 'Remembered';
        } else {
            toast.classList.add('bg-rose-600', 'text-white');
            icon.textContent = 'cancel';
            msg.textContent = 'Forgotten';
        }

        // Pop in
        setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove('translate-y-12', 'opacity-0'), 10);
        // Fade out
        setTimeout(() => toast.classList.add('translate-y-12', 'opacity-0'), 1200);
    }

    // 3. Backward/Forward Queue Navigation
    function prevCard() {
        if (currentIndex > 0) {
            currentIndex--;
            renderCard();
        }
    }

    function skipToNext() {
        // Allows skipping forward without rating IF it was already rated
        if (ratings[queue[currentIndex].id]) {
            currentIndex++;
            renderCard();
        }
    }

    // 4. Batch Save & Session End
    async function saveSession() {
        const res = await fetch('../api/save-session.php', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
                deck_id: data.deckId,
                results: ratings,
                csrf_token: csrfToken,
                duration_seconds: sessionDuration
            })
        });
        let body = {};
        try { body = await res.json(); } catch (_) { }
        if (!res.ok || !body.ok) {
            throw new Error(body.error || `Save failed (HTTP ${res.status}).`);
        }
        unsavedRatings = false;
        return body;
    }

    function showSaveError(message) {
        let box = document.getElementById('save-status');
        if (!box) {
            box = document.createElement('div');
            box.id = 'save-status';
            box.className = 'w-full max-w-sm mb-6 p-4 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 text-sm flex flex-col gap-3';
            viewFinished.insertBefore(box, viewFinished.querySelector('a[href="review.php"]'));
        }
        box.textContent = '';

        const p = document.createElement('p');
        p.textContent = `Your session wasn't saved: ${message}`;

        const btn = document.createElement('button');
        btn.type = 'button';
        btn.className = 'px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white font-semibold';
        btn.textContent = 'Retry save';
        btn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            btn.disabled = true;
            try { await saveSession(); box.remove(); }
            catch (err) { showSaveError(err.message); }
        });

        box.append(p, btn);
    }

    async function endSession() {
        if (sessionEnded) return;
        sessionEnded = true;
        sessionDuration = Math.round((Date.now() - sessionStartedAt) / 1000);
        viewActive.classList.add('hidden');
        viewFinished.classList.remove('hidden');

        const ratedIds = Object.keys(ratings);
        if (ratedIds.length === 0) return;

        // Display Summary
        let remCount = 0;
        Object.values(ratings).forEach(r => r === 'remembered' && remCount++);

        document.getElementById('session-summary-stats').classList.remove('hidden');
        document.getElementById('summary-total').textContent = ratedIds.length;
        document.getElementById('summary-accuracy').textContent = Math.round((remCount / ratedIds.length) * 100) + '%';

        try {
            await saveSession();
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Failed to save session batch:", error);
            showSaveError(error.message);
        }
    }

    // 5. Event Listeners
    btnShow.addEventListener('click', showAnswer);
    btnForgot.addEventListener('click', () => rateCard('forgotten'));
    btnRemember.addEventListener('click', () => rateCard('remembered'));
    document.getElementById('end-session-btn').addEventListener('click', endSession);

    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
        if (sessionEnded) return;

        switch (e.key) {
            case ' ':
                e.preventDefault();
                if (!isAnswerShown) showAnswer();
                else rateCard('remembered'); // Second spacebar defaults to remember & advance
                break;
            case 'f':
            case 'F':
                if (isAnswerShown) rateCard('forgotten');
                break;
            case 'ArrowLeft':
                prevCard();
                break;
            case 'ArrowRight':
                skipToNext();
                break;
        }
    });

    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', (e) => {
        if (unsavedRatings) { e.preventDefault(); e.returnValue = ''; }
    });

    // Utilities
    function escapeHtml(text) {
        const div = document.createElement('div');
        div.textContent = text;
        return div.innerHTML;
    }

    // Init
    renderCard();
});
```

decks.js
```
/**
 * MemPulse Decks Module
 * Handles view toggles, kebab menu dropdowns, modal dialogs, and AJAX actions.
 */
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    const config = window.MemPulseDeckData || {};
    const CSRF_TOKEN = config.csrfToken || '';

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. View toggle (grid vs list), remembered in localStorage
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    const viewGridBtn = document.getElementById('view-grid-btn');
    const viewListBtn = document.getElementById('view-list-btn');
    const gridView = document.getElementById('decks-grid-view');
    const listView = document.getElementById('decks-list-view');

    const VIEW_KEY = 'mempulse_decks_view';
    const activeBtn = "flex items-center justify-center p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-surface-container-lowest text-primary font-semibold shadow-xs transition-all";
    const idleBtn = "flex items-center justify-center p-1.5 rounded-lg text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors";

    function setView(mode) {
        const isGrid = mode !== 'list';
        if (gridView) gridView.classList.toggle('hidden', !isGrid);
        if (listView) listView.classList.toggle('hidden', isGrid);
        if (viewGridBtn) {
            viewGridBtn.className = isGrid ? activeBtn : idleBtn;
            viewGridBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(isGrid));
        }
        if (viewListBtn) {
            viewListBtn.className = isGrid ? idleBtn : activeBtn;
            viewListBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(!isGrid));
        }
    }

    function saveView(mode) {
        try { localStorage.setItem(VIEW_KEY, mode); } catch (_) { }
    }

    if (viewGridBtn && viewListBtn && gridView && listView) {
        let saved = null;
        try { saved = localStorage.getItem(VIEW_KEY); } catch (_) { }
        setView(saved === 'list' ? 'list' : 'grid');

        viewGridBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { setView('grid'); saveView('grid'); });
        viewListBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { setView('list'); saveView('list'); });
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    // Helper: Toast Notifications inside Archived Modal
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    const archivedToast = document.getElementById('archived-toast');
    const archivedToastMsg = document.getElementById('archived-toast-message');

    function showArchivedToast(message, isSuccess = true) {
        if (!archivedToast || !archivedToastMsg) return;
        archivedToastMsg.textContent = message;
        archivedToast.className = isSuccess
            ? "px-4 py-3 rounded-xl font-semibold text-sm transition-all flex items-center justify-between bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200"
            : "px-4 py-3 rounded-xl font-semibold text-sm transition-all flex items-center justify-between bg-rose-50 text-rose-800 border border-rose-200";
        archivedToast.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    // Trigger pulse highlight animation on Archived Decks button
    function triggerArchiveButtonPulse() {
        const btn = document.getElementById('open-archived-btn');
        if (!btn) return;

        btn.classList.add('animate-archive-pulse', 'bg-primary-container', 'text-on-primary-container', 'ring-2', 'ring-primary/40');
        setTimeout(() => {
            btn.classList.remove('animate-archive-pulse', 'bg-primary-container', 'text-on-primary-container', 'ring-2', 'ring-primary/40');
        }, 1500);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. Deck Kebab Menu & Modals Logic
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    const deckMenu = document.getElementById('deck-menu');
    const renameDialog = document.getElementById('rename-dialog');
    const renameForm = document.getElementById('rename-form');
    const renameInput = document.getElementById('rename-input');
    const renameError = document.getElementById('rename-error');
    const renameSave = document.getElementById('rename-save');

    const archiveDialog = document.getElementById('archive-dialog') || document.getElementById('delete-dialog');
    const archiveName = document.getElementById('archive-name') || document.getElementById('delete-name');
    const archiveError = document.getElementById('archive-error') || document.getElementById('delete-error');
    const archiveConfirm = document.getElementById('archive-confirm') || document.getElementById('delete-confirm');
    const countBadge = document.getElementById('deck-count-badge');

    let activeDeckId = null;
    let menuButton = null;

    const archivedDecksDialog = document.getElementById('archived-decks-dialog');
    const openArchivedBtn = document.getElementById('open-archived-btn');

    if (openArchivedBtn && archivedDecksDialog) {
        openArchivedBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            archivedDecksDialog.showModal();
        });
    }

    // Restore Deck Action Handler
    document.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
        const restoreBtn = e.target.closest('[data-restore-deck]');
        if (!restoreBtn) return;

        const deckId = restoreBtn.getAttribute('data-restore-deck');
        const itemEl = document.querySelector(`[data-archived-item="${deckId}"]`);
        const deckTitleEl = document.querySelector(`[data-archived-title="${deckId}"]`);
        const deckTitle = deckTitleEl ? deckTitleEl.textContent.trim() : 'Deck';

        restoreBtn.disabled = true;
        restoreBtn.textContent = "Restoring...";

        try {
            await sendDeckAction({ action: 'restore', deck_id: deckId });

            if (itemEl) itemEl.remove();

            const listContainer = document.getElementById('archived-decks-list-container');
            if (listContainer && listContainer.children.length === 0) {
                listContainer.innerHTML = '<p id="no-archived-msg" class="text-on-surface-variant text-center py-6 text-sm">No archived decks found.</p>';
            }

            showArchivedToast(`"${deckTitle}" has been restored to active decks.`, true);
            window.needMainReload = true;
        } catch (err) {
            showArchivedToast("Failed to restore deck: " + err.message, false);
            restoreBtn.disabled = false;
            restoreBtn.textContent = "Restore";
        }
    });

    // Permanent Delete Handler for Archived Decks
    const permDeleteDialog = document.getElementById('permanent-delete-dialog');
    const permDeleteName = document.getElementById('perm-delete-name');
    const permDeleteError = document.getElementById('perm-delete-error');
    const permDeleteConfirm = document.getElementById('perm-delete-confirm');
    let targetDeleteDeckId = null;

    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const deleteBtn = e.target.closest('[data-delete-archived]');
        if (!deleteBtn) return;

        targetDeleteDeckId = deleteBtn.getAttribute('data-delete-archived');
        const deckTitleEl = document.querySelector(`[data-archived-title="${targetDeleteDeckId}"]`);
        const deckTitle = deckTitleEl ? deckTitleEl.textContent.trim() : 'Deck';

        if (permDeleteName) permDeleteName.textContent = `"${deckTitle}"`;
        if (permDeleteError) permDeleteError.classList.add('hidden');
        if (permDeleteDialog) permDeleteDialog.showModal();
    });

    if (permDeleteConfirm) {
        permDeleteConfirm.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            if (!targetDeleteDeckId) return;

            permDeleteConfirm.disabled = true;
            try {
                const deckTitleEl = document.querySelector(`[data-archived-title="${targetDeleteDeckId}"]`);
                const deckTitle = deckTitleEl ? deckTitleEl.textContent.trim() : 'Deck';

                await sendDeckAction({ action: 'permanent_delete', deck_id: targetDeleteDeckId });

                const itemEl = document.querySelector(`[data-archived-item="${targetDeleteDeckId}"]`);
                if (itemEl) itemEl.remove();

                const listContainer = document.getElementById('archived-decks-list-container');
                if (listContainer && listContainer.children.length === 0) {
                    listContainer.innerHTML = '<p id="no-archived-msg" class="text-on-surface-variant text-center py-6 text-sm">No archived decks found.</p>';
                }

                if (permDeleteDialog) permDeleteDialog.close();
                showArchivedToast(`"${deckTitle}" has been permanently deleted.`, false);
            } catch (err) {
                if (permDeleteError) {
                    permDeleteError.textContent = err.message;
                    permDeleteError.classList.remove('hidden');
                }
            } finally {
                permDeleteConfirm.disabled = false;
            }
        });
    }

    if (archivedDecksDialog) {
        archivedDecksDialog.addEventListener('close', () => {
            if (window.needMainReload) {
                window.location.reload();
            }
        });
    }

    function getDeckTitle(id) {
        const el = document.querySelector('[data-deck-title="' + id + '"]');
        return el ? el.textContent.trim() : '';
    }

    function closeMenu() {
        if (!deckMenu) return;
        deckMenu.classList.add('hidden');
        if (menuButton) menuButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
        menuButton = null;
    }

    function openMenu(btn) {
        closeMenu();
        if (!deckMenu) return;
        menuButton = btn;
        activeDeckId = btn.dataset.deckId;
        btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
        deckMenu.classList.remove('hidden');

        const rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
        const w = deckMenu.offsetWidth;
        const h = deckMenu.offsetHeight;
        const left = Math.min(Math.max(8, rect.right - w), window.innerWidth - w - 8);
        let top = rect.bottom + 6;
        if (top + h > window.innerHeight - 8) top = rect.top - h - 6;
        deckMenu.style.left = left + 'px';
        deckMenu.style.top = Math.max(8, top) + 'px';
    }

    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const btn = e.target.closest('[data-deck-menu]');
        if (btn) {
            if (menuButton === btn) closeMenu(); else openMenu(btn);
        } else if (deckMenu && !deckMenu.contains(e.target)) {
            closeMenu();
        }
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') closeMenu(); });
    window.addEventListener('resize', closeMenu);
    window.addEventListener('scroll', closeMenu, true);

    document.querySelectorAll('[data-close-dialog]').forEach((b) => {
        b.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const dlg = b.closest('dialog');
            if (dlg) dlg.close();
        });
    });

    async function sendDeckAction(payload) {
        const res = await fetch('../api/deck-actions.php', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ ...payload, csrf_token: CSRF_TOKEN })
        });
        let data = {};
        try { data = await res.json(); } catch (_) { }
        if (!res.ok || !data.ok) {
            const err = new Error(data.error || 'Request failed (HTTP ' + res.status + ').');
            err.suggestion = data.suggestion || null;
            throw err;
        }
        return data;
    }

    if (deckMenu) {
        deckMenu.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const item = e.target.closest('[data-menu-action]');
            if (!item || !activeDeckId) return;
            const action = item.dataset.menuAction;
            closeMenu();

            if (action === 'rename' && renameDialog) {
                if (renameInput) renameInput.value = getDeckTitle(activeDeckId);
                if (renameError) renameError.classList.add('hidden');
                renameDialog.showModal();
                if (renameInput) renameInput.select();
            } else if ((action === 'delete' || action === 'archive') && archiveDialog) {
                if (archiveName) archiveName.textContent = '"' + getDeckTitle(activeDeckId) + '"';
                if (archiveError) archiveError.classList.add('hidden');
                archiveDialog.showModal();
            } else if (action === 'add-card') {
                openCardDialog(activeDeckId);
            } else if (action === 'edit-banner' && bannerDialog) {
                selectedBannerPath = null;
                if (bannerError) bannerError.classList.add('hidden');

                // Highlight current selection if any
                document.querySelectorAll('.banner-option').forEach(b => {
                    b.classList.remove('border-primary');
                    b.classList.add('border-transparent');
                });

                bannerDialog.showModal();
            }
        });
    }

    if (renameForm) {
        renameForm.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const title = renameInput ? renameInput.value.trim() : '';
            if (title === '') {
                if (renameError) {
                    renameError.textContent = 'Deck name cannot be empty.';
                    renameError.classList.remove('hidden');
                }
                return;
            }
            if (renameSave) renameSave.disabled = true;
            try {
                const data = await sendDeckAction({ action: 'rename', deck_id: activeDeckId, title });
                document.querySelectorAll('[data-deck-title="' + activeDeckId + '"]').forEach((el) => {
                    el.textContent = data.title;
                });
                if (renameDialog) renameDialog.close();
            } catch (err) {
                showFieldError(renameError, renameInput, err);
            } finally {
                if (renameSave) renameSave.disabled = false;
            }
        });
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. Inline Archiving Action (Animates Card Out & Updates Archive List)
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    if (archiveConfirm) {
        archiveConfirm.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            archiveConfirm.disabled = true;
            try {
                const action = archiveConfirm.id === 'delete-confirm' ? 'delete' : 'archive';
                const deckTitle = getDeckTitle(activeDeckId) || 'Deck';

                // Get metadata from active card element
                const activeCard = document.querySelector(`[data-deck-card="${activeDeckId}"]`);
                let cardCount = '0';
                let accuracy = 'N/A';

                if (activeCard) {
                    const cardCountEl = activeCard.querySelector('.font-semibold.text-on-surface');
                    if (cardCountEl) cardCount = cardCountEl.textContent.trim();

                    // Safely find the accuracy badge element without invalid query selector syntax
                    const accEl = activeCard.querySelector('[class*="badge"]') ||
                        Array.from(activeCard.querySelectorAll('span')).find(el => el.textContent.includes('%'));
                    if (accEl) accuracy = accEl.textContent.trim();
                }

                await sendDeckAction({ action, deck_id: activeDeckId });

                if (archiveDialog) archiveDialog.close();

                // Fade out card elements in grid & list view
                const cardsToRemove = document.querySelectorAll(`[data-deck-card="${activeDeckId}"]`);
                cardsToRemove.forEach(card => {
                    card.style.transition = 'all 0.3s ease';
                    card.style.opacity = '0';
                    card.style.transform = 'scale(0.95)';
                    setTimeout(() => card.remove(), 300);
                });

                // Dynamically insert into Archived Decks Modal
                const listContainer = document.getElementById('archived-decks-list-container');
                if (listContainer) {
                    const noMsg = document.getElementById('no-archived-msg');
                    if (noMsg) noMsg.remove();

                    const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' });
                    const newItemHTML = `
                        <div data-archived-item="${activeDeckId}" class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-xl border border-outline-variant/40 bg-surface gap-4 hover:border-primary/50 transition-colors">
                            <div>
                                <h4 class="font-bold text-on-surface text-[15px]" data-archived-title="${activeDeckId}">${deckTitle}</h4>
                                <div class="text-[13px] text-on-surface-variant mt-1.5 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-x-6 gap-y-1">
                                    <span><strong>Cards:</strong> ${cardCount}</span>
                                    <span><strong>Accuracy:</strong> ${accuracy}</span>
                                    <span><strong>Archived:</strong> ${today}</span>
                                    <span><strong>Reviewed:</strong> Just now</span>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-shrink-0">
                                <button type="button" data-restore-deck="${activeDeckId}" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-primary-container text-on-primary-container hover:bg-primary hover:text-on-primary text-sm font-semibold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shadow-sm">
                                    Restore
                                </button>
                                <button type="button" data-delete-archived="${activeDeckId}" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-50 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap" title="Delete Permanently">
                                    Delete
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>`;
                    listContainer.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', newItemHTML);
                }

                // Trigger Archive Button Highlight Animation
                triggerArchiveButtonPulse();

                // Update remaining deck badge count
                setTimeout(() => {
                    const remaining = document.querySelectorAll('#decks-grid-view [data-deck-card]').length;
                    if (remaining === 0) {
                        window.location.reload();
                    } else if (countBadge) {
                        countBadge.textContent = remaining + (remaining === 1 ? ' Deck' : ' Decks');
                    }
                }, 350);

            } catch (err) {
                if (archiveError) {
                    archiveError.textContent = err.message;
                    archiveError.classList.remove('hidden');
                }
            } finally {
                archiveConfirm.disabled = false;
            }
        });
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. Create Deck Dialog Handler
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    const createDialog = document.getElementById('create-dialog');
    const createForm = document.getElementById('create-form');
    const createInput = document.getElementById('create-input');
    const createError = document.getElementById('create-error');
    const createSave = document.getElementById('create-save');

    let creatingDeckForCard = false;
    let suppressCardResume = false;

    function openCreateDialog(fromCard = false) {
        creatingDeckForCard = fromCard;

        if (fromCard && typeof cardDialog !== 'undefined' && cardDialog && cardDialog.open) {
            suppressCardResume = true;
            cardDialog.close();
            suppressCardResume = false;
        }

        if (createInput) createInput.value = '';
        if (createError) createError.classList.add('hidden');
        if (createSave) createSave.disabled = false;
        if (createDialog) {
            createDialog.showModal();
            if (createInput) createInput.focus();
        }
    }

    ['new-deck-btn', 'new-deck-tile', 'new-deck-row', 'new-deck-empty-grid', 'new-deck-empty-list'].forEach((id) => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) el.addEventListener('click', () => openCreateDialog(false));
    });

    if (createForm) {
        createForm.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const title = createInput ? createInput.value.trim() : '';
            if (title === '') {
                if (createError) {
                    createError.textContent = 'Deck name cannot be empty.';
                    createError.classList.remove('hidden');
                }
                return;
            }
            if (createSave) createSave.disabled = true;
            try {
                const data = await sendDeckAction({ action: 'create', title });

                if (creatingDeckForCard) {
                    window.location.href = 'decks.php?add_card=1&deck_id=' + encodeURIComponent(data.id);
                } else {
                    window.location.reload();
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showFieldError(createError, createInput, err);
                if (createSave) createSave.disabled = false;
            }
        });
    }

    if (createDialog) {
        createDialog.addEventListener('close', () => {
            if (creatingDeckForCard && !suppressCardResume) {
                creatingDeckForCard = false;
                if (typeof cardDeckSelect !== 'undefined' && cardDeckSelect) {
                    cardDeckSelect.value = cardDeckId ? String(cardDeckId) : '';
                }
                if (typeof cardDialog !== 'undefined' && cardDialog) {
                    cardDialog.showModal();
                    if (cardFront) cardFront.focus();
                }
            }
        });
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    // 5. Review Button Handler
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const btn = e.target.closest('[data-action="review"]');
        if (!btn) return;

        const deckId = btn.dataset.deckId;
        if (deckId) {
            window.location.href = 'review.php?deck_id=' + encodeURIComponent(deckId);
        }
    });

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    // 6. Add Card Modal Handler
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    const cardDialog = document.getElementById('card-dialog');
    const cardForm = document.getElementById('card-form');
    const cardDeckSelect = document.getElementById('card-deck-select');
    const cardFront = document.getElementById('card-front');
    const cardBack = document.getElementById('card-back');
    const cardError = document.getElementById('card-error');
    const cardSuccess = document.getElementById('card-success');
    const cardSave = document.getElementById('card-save');

    let cardDeckId = null;
    let cardsAdded = false;

    function openCardDialog(deckId = null) {
        if (!cardDialog) return;
        const selectedId = Number(deckId);
        cardDeckId = Number.isInteger(selectedId) && selectedId > 0 ? selectedId : null;
        cardsAdded = false;

        if (cardDeckSelect) cardDeckSelect.value = cardDeckId ? String(cardDeckId) : '';
        if (cardFront) cardFront.value = '';
        if (cardBack) cardBack.value = '';
        if (cardError) cardError.classList.add('hidden');
        if (cardSuccess) cardSuccess.classList.add('hidden');
        if (cardSave) cardSave.disabled = false;

        cardDialog.showModal();

        if (cardDeckId && cardFront) {
            cardFront.focus();
        } else if (cardDeckSelect) {
            cardDeckSelect.focus();
        }
    }

    if (cardDeckSelect) {
        cardDeckSelect.addEventListener('change', () => {
            if (cardDeckSelect.value === '__create__') {
                cardDeckSelect.value = cardDeckId ? String(cardDeckId) : '';
                openCreateDialog(true);
                return;
            }

            const selectedId = Number(cardDeckSelect.value);
            cardDeckId = Number.isInteger(selectedId) && selectedId > 0 ? selectedId : null;
            if (cardError) cardError.classList.add('hidden');
        });
    }

    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const btn = e.target.closest('[data-action="add-card"]');
        if (btn) openCardDialog(btn.dataset.deckId);
    });

    [cardFront, cardBack].forEach((field) => {
        if (field) {
            field.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter' && (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey)) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if (cardForm) cardForm.requestSubmit();
                }
            });
        }
    });

    if (cardForm) {
        cardForm.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const front = cardFront ? cardFront.value.trim() : '';
            const back = cardBack ? cardBack.value.trim() : '';
            const selectedDeckId = cardDeckSelect ? Number(cardDeckSelect.value) : null;

            if (cardSuccess) cardSuccess.classList.add('hidden');

            if (!Number.isInteger(selectedDeckId) || selectedDeckId <= 0) {
                cardDeckId = null;
                if (cardError) {
                    cardError.textContent = 'Please choose a deck before adding the card.';
                    cardError.classList.remove('hidden');
                }
                if (cardDeckSelect) cardDeckSelect.focus();
                return;
            }

            cardDeckId = selectedDeckId;

            if (front === '' || back === '') {
                if (cardError) {
                    cardError.textContent = 'Both the front and back are required.';
                    cardError.classList.remove('hidden');
                }
                return;
            }
            if (cardError) cardError.classList.add('hidden');

            if (cardSave) cardSave.disabled = true;
            try {
                await sendDeckAction({ action: 'add_card', deck_id: cardDeckId, front, back });
                cardsAdded = true;
                if (cardFront) cardFront.value = '';
                if (cardBack) cardBack.value = '';
                if (cardSuccess) cardSuccess.classList.remove('hidden');
                if (cardFront) cardFront.focus();
            } catch (err) {
                if (cardError) {
                    cardError.textContent = err.message;
                    cardError.classList.remove('hidden');
                }
            } finally {
                if (cardSave) cardSave.disabled = false;
            }
        });
    }

    function showFieldError(errorEl, input, err) {
        if (!errorEl) return;
        errorEl.textContent = err.message;
        if (err.suggestion && input) {
            const btn = document.createElement('button');
            btn.type = 'button';
            btn.className = 'ml-2 underline font-semibold';
            btn.textContent = 'Use "' + err.suggestion + '"';
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                input.value = err.suggestion;
                errorEl.classList.add('hidden');
                input.focus();
            });
            errorEl.appendChild(btn);
        }
        errorEl.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    if (cardDialog) {
        cardDialog.addEventListener('close', () => {
            if (cardsAdded) {
                const cleanUrl = window.location.pathname;
                window.history.replaceState({}, document.title, cleanUrl);
                window.location.reload();
            }
        });
    }

    // Auto-open modals via URL params
    if (config.createRequested) {
        openCreateDialog();
    } else if (config.addCardRequested) {
        openCardDialog(config.requestedDeckId);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    // 7. Custom Deck Position Management via LocalStorage
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    const ORDER_KEY = 'mempulse_deck_order';
    const gridContainer = document.getElementById('decks-grid-view');
    const listContainer = document.getElementById('decks-list-view');

    const movePosDialog = document.getElementById('move-pos-dialog');
    const movePosForm = document.getElementById('move-pos-form');
    const movePosName = document.getElementById('move-pos-deck-name');
    const movePosInput = document.getElementById('move-pos-input');
    const movePosHint = document.getElementById('move-pos-hint');

    function getDeckElements(container) {
        if (!container) return [];
        return Array.from(container.querySelectorAll('.deck-card[data-deck-id]'));
    }

    function applySavedDeckOrder(container) {
        if (!container) return;

        let savedOrder = null;
        try {
            savedOrder = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ORDER_KEY));
        } catch (_) { }

        if (!Array.isArray(savedOrder) || savedOrder.length === 0) return;

        const items = getDeckElements(container);
        const itemMap = new Map();
        items.forEach(item => itemMap.set(String(item.dataset.deckId), item));

        savedOrder.forEach(deckId => {
            const el = itemMap.get(String(deckId));
            if (el) {
                container.appendChild(el);
                itemMap.delete(String(deckId));
            }
        });

        const createBtn = container.querySelector('#new-deck-tile, #new-deck-row');
        itemMap.forEach(el => {
            if (createBtn) container.insertBefore(el, createBtn);
            else container.appendChild(el);
        });

        if (createBtn) container.appendChild(createBtn);
    }

    function saveCurrentDeckOrder(container) {
        if (!container) return;
        const items = getDeckElements(container);
        const order = items.map(el => Number(el.dataset.deckId));
        try {
            localStorage.setItem(ORDER_KEY, JSON.stringify(order));
        } catch (_) { }
    }

    function moveDeckToPosition(targetDeckId, newPos) {
        [gridContainer, listContainer].forEach(container => {
            if (!container) return;
            const items = getDeckElements(container);
            const targetEl = container.querySelector(`.deck-card[data-deck-id="${targetDeckId}"]`);
            if (!targetEl || items.length === 0) return;

            const targetIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(newPos - 1, items.length - 1));
            const createBtn = container.querySelector('#new-deck-tile, #new-deck-row');

            const remaining = items.filter(el => String(el.dataset.deckId) !== String(targetDeckId));
            remaining.splice(targetIdx, 0, targetEl);

            remaining.forEach(el => {
                if (createBtn) container.insertBefore(el, createBtn);
                else container.appendChild(el);
            });
            if (createBtn) container.appendChild(createBtn);

            saveCurrentDeckOrder(container);
        });
    }

    // Apply saved order on load
    applySavedDeckOrder(gridContainer);
    applySavedDeckOrder(listContainer);

    // Kebab Menu Handler for Move Position
    if (deckMenu) {
        deckMenu.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const item = e.target.closest('[data-menu-action="move-pos"]');
            if (!item || !activeDeckId) return;

            const deckTitle = getDeckTitle(activeDeckId) || 'Deck';
            const currentItems = getDeckElements(gridContainer || listContainer);
            const currentIdx = currentItems.findIndex(el => String(el.dataset.deckId) === String(activeDeckId));

            if (movePosName) movePosName.textContent = `"${deckTitle}"`;
            if (movePosInput) {
                movePosInput.value = currentIdx !== -1 ? currentIdx + 1 : 1;
                movePosInput.max = currentItems.length;
            }
            if (movePosHint) movePosHint.textContent = `Enter position between 1 and ${currentItems.length}.`;

            if (movePosDialog) movePosDialog.showModal();
        });
    }

    if (movePosForm) {
        movePosForm.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const newPos = parseInt(movePosInput ? movePosInput.value : '1', 10);
            if (!isNaN(newPos) && activeDeckId) {
                moveDeckToPosition(activeDeckId, newPos);
                if (movePosDialog) movePosDialog.close();
            }
        });
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    // Edit Banner Modal Handler
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    const bannerDialog = document.getElementById('banner-dialog');
    const bannerSave = document.getElementById('banner-save');
    const bannerError = document.getElementById('banner-error');
    let selectedBannerPath = null;

    // Delegate banner image clicks inside the dialog
    if (bannerDialog) {
        bannerDialog.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const optionBtn = e.target.closest('.banner-option');
            if (!optionBtn) return;

            document.querySelectorAll('.banner-option').forEach(b => {
                b.classList.remove('border-primary');
                b.classList.add('border-transparent');
            });

            optionBtn.classList.remove('border-transparent');
            optionBtn.classList.add('border-primary');
            selectedBannerPath = optionBtn.dataset.bannerPath;
        });
    }

    if (bannerSave) {
        bannerSave.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            if (selectedBannerPath === null) {
                if (bannerError) {
                    bannerError.textContent = "Please select a banner image or default gradient.";
                    bannerError.classList.remove('hidden');
                }
                return;
            }

            bannerSave.disabled = true;
            try {
```
