DashboardService.php
```
<?php
/**
 * DashboardService
 * 
 * Centralized service layer for handling database reads, user profiling,
 * spaced repetition metric analytics, and streak tracking.
 */
class DashboardService
{
    /**
     * Retrieve authenticated user profile details.
     * 
     * @param mysqli $conn Active database connection instance.
     * @param int $userId Authenticated user ID.
     * @return array Profile metadata containing display name.
     */
    public static function getUserProfile(mysqli $conn, int $userId): array
    {
        $sql = "SELECT first_name, last_name FROM users WHERE id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL";
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql);
        
        if (!$stmt) {
            return ['displayName' => 'Active Learner'];
        }

        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $userId);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        mysqli_stmt_bind_result($stmt, $firstName, $lastName);
        mysqli_stmt_fetch($stmt);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        $fullName = trim(($firstName ?? '') . ' ' . ($lastName ?? ''));
        
        return [
            'displayName' => !empty($fullName) ? $fullName : 'Active Learner'
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Fetch core flashcard and study session metrics.
     * 
     * @param mysqli $conn Active database connection instance.
     * @param int $userId Authenticated user ID.
     * @return array Calculated user metrics.
     */
    public static function getMetrics(mysqli $conn, int $userId): array
    {
        // 1. Cards Due Today
        $due = self::fetchCount($conn, 
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM flashcards f 
             JOIN decks d ON f.deck_id = d.id
             WHERE d.user_id = ? AND d.deleted_at IS NULL AND f.deleted_at IS NULL AND f.next_review_at <= NOW()", 
            $userId
        );

        // 2. Mastered Cards (Interval >= 21 days)
        $mastered = self::fetchCount($conn, 
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM flashcards f 
             JOIN decks d ON f.deck_id = d.id 
             WHERE d.user_id = ? AND d.deleted_at IS NULL AND f.deleted_at IS NULL AND f.interval_days >= 21", 
            $userId
        );

        // 3. Tough Cards / Leeches (Lapses >= 8)
        $tough = self::fetchCount($conn, 
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM flashcards f 
             JOIN decks d ON f.deck_id = d.id 
             WHERE d.user_id = ? AND d.deleted_at IS NULL AND f.deleted_at IS NULL AND f.lapses >= 8", 
            $userId
        );

        // 4. Cards Reviewed Today
        $reviewedToday = self::fetchCount($conn, 
            "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total_reviewed), 0) FROM review_sessions 
             WHERE user_id = ? AND ended_at IS NOT NULL AND DATE(started_at) = CURDATE()", 
            $userId
        );

        // 5. Retention Rate Percentage
        $retentionRate = 0.0;
        $retentionSql = "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(remembered_count), 0), COALESCE(SUM(total_reviewed), 0) 
                         FROM review_sessions 
                         WHERE user_id = ? AND ended_at IS NOT NULL";
                         
        if ($stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $retentionSql)) {
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $userId);
            mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
            mysqli_stmt_bind_result($stmt, $remembered, $reviewed);
            if (mysqli_stmt_fetch($stmt) && $reviewed > 0) {
                $retentionRate = round(($remembered / $reviewed) * 100, 1);
            }
            mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
        }

        return [
            'cardsDue' => $due,
            'masteredCards' => $mastered,
            'toughCards' => $tough,
            'cardsReviewedToday' => $reviewedToday,
            'retentionRate' => $retentionRate
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Compute activity history, streaks, and heatmap data.
     * 
     * @param mysqli $conn Active database connection instance.
     * @param int $userId Authenticated user ID.
     * @return array Streak counters and calendar heatmap data.
     */
    public static function getStreakAndHeatmapData(mysqli $conn, int $userId): array
    {
        $sessionSql = "SELECT DATE(started_at) as session_date, 
                              SUM(remembered_count) as total_remembered, 
                              SUM(total_reviewed) as total_reviewed
                       FROM review_sessions 
                       WHERE user_id = ? AND ended_at IS NOT NULL
                       GROUP BY DATE(started_at)
                       HAVING SUM(total_reviewed) > 0
                       ORDER BY session_date DESC";

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sessionSql);
        if (!$stmt) {
            return self::emptyStreakState();
        }

        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $userId);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);

        $activityHistory = [];
        while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
            $activityHistory[$row['session_date']] = [
                'remembered' => (int) $row['total_remembered'],
                'reviewed' => (int) $row['total_reviewed']
            ];
        }
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        if (empty($activityHistory)) {
            return self::emptyStreakState();
        }

        // Streak Analytics Processing
        $todayStr = date('Y-m-d');
        $yesterdayStr = date('Y-m-d', strtotime('-1 day'));
        
        $currentStreak = 0;
        $bestStreak = 0;
        $streakAccuracy = 0.0;
        
        $dates = array_keys($activityHistory);
        $latestDate = $dates[0];

        // Active Streak calculation
        if ($latestDate === $todayStr || $latestDate === $yesterdayStr) {
            $checkDate = new DateTime($latestDate);
            $streakRemembered = 0;
            $streakReviewed = 0;

            while (true) {
                $dateKey = $checkDate->format('Y-m-d');
                if (isset($activityHistory[$dateKey])) {
                    $currentStreak++;
                    $streakRemembered += $activityHistory[$dateKey]['remembered'];
                    $streakReviewed += $activityHistory[$dateKey]['reviewed'];
                    $checkDate->modify('-1 day');
                } else {
                    break;
                }
            }

            if ($streakReviewed > 0) {
                $streakAccuracy = round(($streakRemembered / $streakReviewed) * 100, 1);
            }
        }

        // Best Historical Streak calculation
        $tempStreak = 0;
        $expectedDate = null;

        foreach (array_reverse($dates) as $dateStr) {
            $currentDate = new DateTime($dateStr);
            if ($expectedDate === null || $currentDate == $expectedDate) {
                $tempStreak++;
            } else {
                if ($tempStreak > $bestStreak) {
                    $bestStreak = $tempStreak;
                }
                $tempStreak = 1;
            }
            $expectedDate = clone $currentDate;
            $expectedDate->modify('+1 day');
        }

        if ($tempStreak > $bestStreak) {
            $bestStreak = $tempStreak;
        }

        // Heatmap bounds calculation
        $heatmapEnd = new DateTime('today');
        $heatmapStart = (clone $heatmapEnd)->modify('-364 days');
        $heatmapData = [];
        $studyDays = 0;

        foreach ($activityHistory as $dateStr => $metrics) {
            $activityDate = DateTime::createFromFormat('!Y-m-d', $dateStr);
            if ($activityDate && $activityDate >= $heatmapStart && $activityDate <= $heatmapEnd && $metrics['reviewed'] > 0) {
                $heatmapData[$dateStr] = $metrics['reviewed'];
                $studyDays++;
            }
        }

        $calendarStart = clone $heatmapStart;
        $calendarStart->modify('monday this week');

        return [
            'currentStreak' => $currentStreak,
            'bestStreak' => $bestStreak,
            'streakAccuracy' => $streakAccuracy,
            'studyDays' => $studyDays,
            'heatmapData' => $heatmapData,
            'heatmapStart' => $heatmapStart,
            'heatmapEnd' => $heatmapEnd,
            'calendarStart' => $calendarStart
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Retrieve recent completed study sessions for the activity feed.
     * 
     * @param mysqli $conn Active database connection instance.
     * @param int $userId Authenticated user ID.
     * @param int $limit Max sessions to return.
     * @return array Session history items.
     */
    public static function getRecentSessions(mysqli $conn, int $userId, int $limit = 4): array
    {
        $sql = "SELECT rs.id, d.title as deck_title, rs.total_reviewed as cards_reviewed, 
                       rs.accuracy_percentage as accuracy, rs.started_at 
                FROM review_sessions rs 
                JOIN decks d ON rs.deck_id = d.id 
                WHERE rs.user_id = ? AND rs.ended_at IS NOT NULL 
                ORDER BY rs.started_at DESC LIMIT ?";

        $sessions = [];
        if ($stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql)) {
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ii", $userId, $limit);
            mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
            $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);

            while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
                $timeDiff = time() - strtotime($row['started_at']);
                if ($timeDiff < 3600) {
                    $formatted = floor($timeDiff / 60) . ' mins ago';
                } elseif ($timeDiff < 86400) {
                    $formatted = floor($timeDiff / 3600) . ' hours ago';
                } else {
                    $formatted = floor($timeDiff / 86400) . ' days ago';
                }

                $sessions[] = [
                    'deckTitle' => $row['deck_title'],
                    'cardsReviewed' => (int) $row['cards_reviewed'],
                    'accuracy' => (int) $row['accuracy'],
                    'formattedTime' => $formatted
                ];
            }
            mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
        }

        return $sessions;
    }

        /**
     * Decks that currently have cards due, most due first.
     *
     * @return array<int, array{id:int,title:string,dueCount:int}>
     */
    public static function getDueByDeck(mysqli $conn, int $userId): array
    {
        $sql = "SELECT d.id, d.title, COUNT(*) AS due_count
                FROM flashcards f
                JOIN decks d ON f.deck_id = d.id
                WHERE d.user_id = ? AND d.deleted_at IS NULL
                  AND f.deleted_at IS NULL AND f.next_review_at <= NOW()
                GROUP BY d.id, d.title
                ORDER BY due_count DESC, d.title ASC";

        $decks = [];
        if ($stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql)) {
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $userId);
            mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
            $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);
            while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
                $decks[] = [
                    'id'       => (int) $row['id'],
                    'title'    => $row['title'],
                    'dueCount' => (int) $row['due_count'],
                ];
            }
            mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
        }
        return $decks;
    }

    /**
     * Seconds until the next card becomes due, or null if the user has no upcoming cards.
     * Computed in SQL so PHP/MySQL timezone differences cannot skew it.
     */
    public static function getNextDueSeconds(mysqli $conn, int $userId): ?int
    {
        $sql = "SELECT TIMESTAMPDIFF(SECOND, NOW(), MIN(f.next_review_at))
                FROM flashcards f
                JOIN decks d ON f.deck_id = d.id
                WHERE d.user_id = ? AND d.deleted_at IS NULL
                  AND f.deleted_at IS NULL AND f.next_review_at > NOW()";

        $secs = null;
        if ($stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql)) {
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $userId);
            mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
            mysqli_stmt_bind_result($stmt, $secs);
            mysqli_stmt_fetch($stmt);
            mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
        }
        return $secs === null ? null : (int) $secs;
    }
    private static function fetchCount(mysqli $conn, string $query, int $userId): int
    {
        if ($stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $query)) {
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $userId);
            mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
            mysqli_stmt_bind_result($stmt, $count);
            mysqli_stmt_fetch($stmt);
            mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
            return (int) $count;
        }
        return 0;
    }

    private static function emptyStreakState(): array
    {
        return [
            'currentStreak' => 0,
            'bestStreak' => 0,
            'streakAccuracy' => 0.0,
            'studyDays' => 0,
            'heatmapData' => [],
            'heatmapStart' => new DateTime('-364 days'),
            'heatmapEnd' => new DateTime('today'),
            'calendarStart' => (new DateTime('-364 days'))->modify('monday this week')
        ];
    }
}
```

deck-actions.php
```
<?php
/**
 * Deck Actions API Endpoint
 * 
 * Secure HTTP POST JSON endpoint handling asynchronous deck and flashcard mutation 
 * actions including creation, renaming, archiving, restoring, permanent deletion, 
 * and flashcard addition.
 */

session_start();
header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8');

/**
 * Send a structured JSON HTTP response and terminate request execution.
 * 
 * @param int $status HTTP status code.
 * @param array $body Response payload data.
 * @return void
 */
function respond(int $status, array $body): void
{
    http_response_code($status);
    echo json_encode($body);
    exit;
}

/**
 * Generate a unique deck title suggestion when a collision occurs.
 * 
 * @param callable $exists Callback function checking title existence.
 * @param string $title Target base deck title.
 * @param int $max Maximum allowed title character length.
 * @return string Available deck title candidate.
 */
function suggest_unique(callable $exists, string $title, int $max = 150): string
{
    $base = preg_replace('/\s*\(\d+\)$/u', '', $title);
    $base = mb_substr($base, 0, $max - 8, 'UTF-8');
    for ($n = 2; $n < 1000; $n++) {
        $candidate = $base . ' (' . $n . ')';
        if (!$exists($candidate)) {
            return $candidate;
        }
    }
    return $base . ' (' . bin2hex(random_bytes(2)) . ')';
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Request Validation & Authentication
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] !== 'POST') {
    respond(405, ['error' => 'Method not allowed.']);
}

if (!isset($_SESSION['logged_in']) || $_SESSION['logged_in'] !== true || !isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    respond(401, ['error' => 'Please log in again.']);
}

$input = json_decode(file_get_contents('php://input'), true);
if (!is_array($input)) {
    respond(400, ['error' => 'Invalid request.']);
}

// Anti-CSRF Token Verification
$token = $input['csrf_token'] ?? '';
if (empty($_SESSION['csrf_token']) || !is_string($token) || !hash_equals($_SESSION['csrf_token'], $token)) {
    respond(403, ['error' => 'Security check failed. Refresh the page and try again.']);
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/db.php';

$user_id = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$deck_id = filter_var($input['deck_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
$action  = $input['action'] ?? '';

/**
 * Helper Closure: Check if a live deck title exists for the authenticated user.
 * 
 * @param string $t Deck title string.
 * @param int $exclude_id Optional deck ID to ignore (for renaming).
 * @return bool True if collision exists, false otherwise.
 */
$deck_exists = function (string $t, int $exclude_id = 0) use ($conn, $user_id): bool {
    $s = mysqli_prepare($conn, "SELECT 1 FROM decks WHERE user_id = ? AND title = ? AND id <> ? AND deleted_at IS NULL LIMIT 1");
    mysqli_stmt_bind_param($s, "isi", $user_id, $t, $exclude_id);
    mysqli_stmt_execute($s);
    mysqli_stmt_store_result($s);
    $found = mysqli_stmt_num_rows($s) > 0;
    mysqli_stmt_close($s);
    return $found;
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Action Processor: Create Deck
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
if ($action === 'create') {
    $title = trim((string) ($input['title'] ?? ''));

    if ($title === '') {
        respond(422, ['error' => 'Deck name cannot be empty.']);
    }
    if (mb_strlen($title, 'UTF-8') > 150) {
        respond(422, ['error' => 'Deck name must be 150 characters or fewer.']);
    }
    
    if ($deck_exists($title)) {
        respond(409, [
            'error' => 'You already have a deck named "' . $title . '".',
            'suggestion' => suggest_unique($deck_exists, $title)
        ]);
    }
    
    try {
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO decks (user_id, title) VALUES (?, ?)");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "is", $user_id, $title);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $new_id = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        respond(200, ['ok' => true, 'id' => $new_id, 'title' => $title]);
    } catch (Throwable $e) {
        error_log('deck-actions.php (create): ' . $e->getMessage());
        respond(500, ['error' => 'Something went wrong. Please try again.']);
    }
}

// Validate Deck ID for deck-specific actions
if (!$deck_id || $deck_id < 1) {
    respond(400, ['error' => 'Invalid deck.']);
}

// Verify User Ownership over the Target Deck
$check = mysqli_prepare($conn, "SELECT id FROM decks WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?");
mysqli_stmt_bind_param($check, "ii", $deck_id, $user_id);
mysqli_stmt_execute($check);
mysqli_stmt_store_result($check);
$owns_deck = mysqli_stmt_num_rows($check) === 1;
mysqli_stmt_close($check);

if (!$owns_deck) {
    respond(404, ['error' => 'Deck not found.']);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Action Processor: Targeted Deck Operations
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
try {
    // Action: Rename Deck
    if ($action === 'rename') {
        $title = trim((string) ($input['title'] ?? ''));

        if ($title === '') {
            respond(422, ['error' => 'Deck name cannot be empty.']);
        }
        if (mb_strlen($title, 'UTF-8') > 150) {
            respond(422, ['error' => 'Deck name must be 150 characters or fewer.']);
        }

        $same_name = fn(string $t): bool => $deck_exists($t, $deck_id);
        if ($same_name($title)) {
            respond(409, [
                'error' => 'You already have a deck named "' . $title . '".',
                'suggestion' => suggest_unique($same_name, $title)
            ]);
        }

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE decks SET title = ? WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "sii", $title, $deck_id, $user_id);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        respond(200, ['ok' => true, 'title' => $title]);
    }

    // Action: Soft Delete / Archive Deck and Flashcards
    if ($action === 'archive') {
        mysqli_begin_transaction($conn);
        
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE decks SET deleted_at = NOW() WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ii", $deck_id, $user_id);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
        
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE flashcards SET deleted_at = NOW() WHERE deck_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $deck_id);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
        
        mysqli_commit($conn);
        respond(200, ['ok' => true]);
    }

    // Action: Restore Archived Deck and Associated Cards
    if ($action === 'restore') {
        mysqli_begin_transaction($conn);

        // Only cards archived together with the deck (not ones archived individually earlier)
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE flashcards f
                                        JOIN decks d ON d.id = f.deck_id
                                        SET f.deleted_at = NULL
                                        WHERE f.deck_id = ? AND d.user_id = ?
                                        AND d.deleted_at IS NOT NULL
                                        AND f.deleted_at >= d.deleted_at");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ii", $deck_id, $user_id);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE decks SET deleted_at = NULL WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NOT NULL");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ii", $deck_id, $user_id);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        mysqli_commit($conn);
        respond(200, ['ok' => true]);
    }
    
    // Action: Permanent Delete Deck and Flashcards
    if ($action === 'permanent_delete') {
        mysqli_begin_transaction($conn);
        
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "DELETE FROM decks WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NOT NULL");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ii", $deck_id, $user_id);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
        
        mysqli_commit($conn);
        respond(200, ['ok' => true]);
    }

    // Action: Add Flashcard to Deck
    if ($action === 'add_card') {
        $front = trim((string) ($input['front'] ?? ''));
        $back  = trim((string) ($input['back'] ?? ''));
        
        if ($front === '' || $back === '') {
            respond(422, ['error' => 'Both the front and back of the card are required.']);
        }
        if (mb_strlen($front, 'UTF-8') > 5000 || mb_strlen($back, 'UTF-8') > 5000) {
            respond(422, ['error' => 'Each side must be 5000 characters or fewer.']);
        }
                
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO flashcards (deck_id, front, back) VALUES (?, ?, ?)");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "iss", $deck_id, $front, $back);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        respond(200, ['ok' => true]);
    }

    // Action: Update Deck Banner Cover
    if ($action === 'update_cover') {
        $cover_image = trim((string) ($input['cover_image'] ?? ''));

        // Validate path security to prevent path traversal (.jpg, .jpeg, .png, .webp)
        if ($cover_image !== '' && !preg_match('/^\.\.\/assets\/images\/deck-header-banner\/[a-zA-Z0-9_\-]+\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp|svg)$/i', $cover_image)) {
            respond(422, ['error' => 'Invalid image selected.']);
        }

        $db_val = $cover_image === '' ? NULL : $cover_image;

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE decks SET cover_image = ? WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "sii", $db_val, $deck_id, $user_id);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        respond(200, ['ok' => true, 'cover_image' => $cover_image]);
    }

    respond(400, ['error' => 'Unknown action.']);
} catch (Throwable $e) {
    if ($action === 'archive' || $action === 'restore') {
        mysqli_rollback($conn);
    }
    error_log('deck-actions.php: ' . $e->getMessage());
    respond(500, ['error' => 'Something went wrong. Please try again.']);
}

DeckService.php
```
<?php
/**
 * DeckService
 * 
 * Centralized service layer for fetching active/archived user decks,
 * computing accuracy metrics, and formatting UI labels.
 */
class DeckService
{
    /**
     * Retrieve active decks for a user with card counts, due counts, and review stats.
     * 
     * @param mysqli $conn Active database connection instance.
     * @param int $userId Authenticated user ID.
     * @return array Array of formatted deck data objects.
     */
    public static function getUserDecks(mysqli $conn, int $userId): array
    {
        $sql = "SELECT d.id,
                       d.title,
                       d.cover_image,
                       (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM flashcards f
                         WHERE f.deck_id = d.id AND f.deleted_at IS NULL) AS card_count,
                       (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM flashcards f
                         WHERE f.deck_id = d.id AND f.deleted_at IS NULL
                           AND f.next_review_at <= NOW()) AS due_count,
                       (SELECT MIN(f.next_review_at) FROM flashcards f
                         WHERE f.deck_id = d.id AND f.deleted_at IS NULL) AS next_due,
                       (SELECT MAX(rs.ended_at) FROM review_sessions rs
                         WHERE rs.deck_id = d.id AND rs.user_id = d.user_id
                           AND rs.ended_at IS NOT NULL) AS last_reviewed,
                       (SELECT SUM(rs.remembered_count) FROM review_sessions rs
                         WHERE rs.deck_id = d.id AND rs.user_id = d.user_id
                           AND rs.ended_at IS NOT NULL) AS remembered,
                       (SELECT SUM(rs.total_reviewed) FROM review_sessions rs
                         WHERE rs.deck_id = d.id AND rs.user_id = d.user_id
                           AND rs.ended_at IS NOT NULL) AS reviewed
                FROM decks d
                WHERE d.user_id = ? AND d.deleted_at IS NULL
                ORDER BY d.created_at DESC";

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql);
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $userId);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);

        $decks = [];
        while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
            $cardCount = (int) $row['card_count'];
            $reviewed  = (int) $row['reviewed'];
            $accuracy  = $reviewed > 0 ? (int) round(((int) $row['remembered'] / $reviewed) * 100) : null;
            $hue       = ((int) $row['id'] * 47) % 360;
            $defaultGradient = "linear-gradient(135deg, hsl({$hue} 65% 45%), hsl(" . (($hue + 45) % 360) . " 65% 28%))";

            if (!empty($row['cover_image'])) {
                $coverStyle = "background-image: url('" .$row['cover_image'] . "'); background-size: cover; background-position: center;";
                $hasCustomImage = true;
            } else {
                $coverStyle = "background: {$defaultGradient};";
                $hasCustomImage = false;
            }

            $decks[] = [
                'id'                 => (int) $row['id'],
                'title'              => $row['title'],
                'cover_image'        => $row['cover_image'],
                'has_custom_image'   => $hasCustomImage,
                'card_count'         => $cardCount,
                'due_count'          => (int) $row['due_count'],
                'accuracy'           => $accuracy,
                'theme'              => self::accuracyTheme($accuracy),
                'due_text'           => self::dueLabel((int) $row['due_count'], $cardCount, $row['next_due']),
                'last_reviewed_text' => self::lastReviewedLabel($row['last_reviewed']),
                'cover_style' => $coverStyle,            
                ];
        }
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        return $decks;
    }

    /**
     * Retrieve archived (soft-deleted) decks for a user.
     * 
     * @param mysqli $conn Active database connection instance.
     * @param int $userId Authenticated user ID.
     * @return array Array of archived deck objects.
     */
    public static function getArchivedDecks(mysqli $conn, int $userId): array
    {
        $sql = "SELECT d.id,
                       d.title,
                       d.deleted_at AS archive_date,
                       (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM flashcards f WHERE f.deck_id = d.id) AS card_count,
                       (SELECT MAX(rs.ended_at) FROM review_sessions rs WHERE rs.deck_id = d.id AND rs.user_id = d.user_id) AS last_reviewed,
                       (SELECT SUM(rs.remembered_count) FROM review_sessions rs WHERE rs.deck_id = d.id AND rs.user_id = d.user_id) AS remembered,
                       (SELECT SUM(rs.total_reviewed) FROM review_sessions rs WHERE rs.deck_id = d.id AND rs.user_id = d.user_id) AS reviewed
                FROM decks d
                WHERE d.user_id = ? AND d.deleted_at IS NOT NULL
                ORDER BY d.deleted_at DESC";

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql);
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $userId);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);

        $archived = [];
        while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
            $reviewed = (int) $row['reviewed'];
            $accuracy = $reviewed > 0 ? (int) round(((int) $row['remembered'] / $reviewed) * 100) : null;
            $row['accuracy'] = $accuracy;
            $archived[] = $row;
        }
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        return $archived;
    }

    /**
     * Escape HTML output safely.
     * 
     * @param mixed $value Input value to escape.
     * @return string Safe HTML entity output.
     */
    public static function h($value): string
    {
        return htmlspecialchars((string) $value, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
    }

    /**
     * Color theme configuration for accuracy badge and progress bar.
     * 
     * @param int|null $pct Accuracy percentage.
     * @return array Class names, icons, and labels.
     */
    public static function accuracyTheme(?int $pct): array
    {
        if ($pct === null) {
            return [
                'badge' => 'bg-surface-container text-on-surface-variant border-outline-variant/40',
                'bar'   => 'bg-outline-variant',
                'icon'  => 'hourglass_empty',
                'label' => 'No reviews yet',
            ];
        }
        if ($pct >= 90) {
            return ['badge' => 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200/80', 'bar' => 'bg-emerald-500', 'icon' => 'verified', 'label' => $pct . '% Accuracy'];
        }
        if ($pct >= 75) {
            return ['badge' => 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200/80', 'bar' => 'bg-amber-500', 'icon' => 'speed', 'label' => $pct . '% Accuracy'];
        }
        return ['badge' => 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200/80', 'bar' => 'bg-rose-500', 'icon' => 'trending_down', 'label' => $pct . '% Accuracy'];
    }

    /**
     * Generate human-readable due status label.
     * 
     * @param int $dueCount Cards due now.
     * @param int $cardCount Total cards in deck.
     * @param string|null $nextDue Timestamp of next due card.
     * @return string Formatted string.
     */
    public static function dueLabel(int $dueCount, int $cardCount, ?string $nextDue): string
    {
        if ($cardCount === 0) {
            return 'No cards yet';
        }
        if ($dueCount > 0) {
            return $dueCount . ' due now';
        }
        if ($nextDue === null) {
            return 'Nothing due';
        }
        $diff = (new DateTime())->diff(new DateTime($nextDue));
        if ($diff->days >= 1) {
            return 'Due in ' . $diff->days . ($diff->days === 1 ? ' day' : ' days');
        }
        if ($diff->h >= 1) {
            return 'Due in ' . $diff->h . ($diff->h === 1 ? ' hr' : ' hrs');
        }
        return 'Due in under 1 hr';
    }

    /**
     * Generate human-readable relative time string for last review.
     * 
     * @param string|null $lastReviewed Timestamp of last review session.
     * @return string Relative time string.
     */
    public static function lastReviewedLabel(?string $lastReviewed): string
    {
        if ($lastReviewed === null) {
            return 'Never reviewed';
        }
        $reviewedDate = new DateTime($lastReviewed);
        $now = new DateTime();
        $diff = $now->diff($reviewedDate);

        if ($diff->days === 0) {
            if ($diff->h === 0) {
                return $diff->i <= 1 ? 'Reviewed just now' : 'Reviewed ' . $diff->i . ' mins ago';
            }
            return 'Reviewed ' . $diff->h . ($diff->h === 1 ? ' hr ago' : ' hrs ago');
        }
        if ($diff->days === 1) {
            return 'Reviewed yesterday';
        }
        if ($diff->days < 30) {
            return 'Reviewed ' . $diff->days . ' days ago';
        }
        return 'Reviewed ' . $reviewedDate->format('M j, Y');
    }
}
```

flashcard-actions.php
```
<?php
/**
 * Flashcard Actions API Endpoint
 * 
 * Secure HTTP POST JSON endpoint handling asynchronous flashcard mutations 
 * including addition, editing, and soft-deletion.
 */

session_start();
header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8');

require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/db.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/FlashcardService.php'; 
require_once __DIR__ . '/ArchiveService.php';   

/**
 * Send a structured JSON HTTP response and terminate request execution.
 * 
 * @param int $status HTTP status code.
 * @param array $body Response payload data.
 * @return void
 */
function respond(int $status, array $body): void
{
    http_response_code($status);
    echo json_encode($body);
    exit;
}

// 1. Request Method Verification
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] !== 'POST') {
    respond(405, ['error' => 'Method not allowed.']);
}

// 2. Authentication Check
if (!isset($_SESSION['logged_in']) || $_SESSION['logged_in'] !== true || !isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    respond(401, ['error' => 'Please log in again.']);
}

// 3. Input Data Parsing
$input = json_decode(file_get_contents('php://input'), true);
if (!is_array($input)) {
    respond(400, ['error' => 'Invalid request structure.']);
}

// 4. Anti-CSRF Token Verification
$token = $input['csrf_token'] ?? '';
if (empty($_SESSION['csrf_token']) || !is_string($token) || !hash_equals($_SESSION['csrf_token'], $token)) {
    respond(403, ['error' => 'Security check failed. Refresh the page and try again.']);
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/db.php';

$user_id = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$action  = $input['action'] ?? '';
$deck_id = filter_var($input['deck_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);

if (!$deck_id || $deck_id < 1) {
    respond(400, ['error' => 'Invalid deck selection.']);
}

// 5. Verify User Ownership of Target Deck
$checkStmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "SELECT id FROM decks WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL");
mysqli_stmt_bind_param($checkStmt, "ii", $deck_id, $user_id);
mysqli_stmt_execute($checkStmt);
mysqli_stmt_store_result($checkStmt);
$ownsDeck = mysqli_stmt_num_rows($checkStmt) === 1;
mysqli_stmt_close($checkStmt);

if (!$ownsDeck) {
    respond(404, ['error' => 'Deck not found or access denied.']);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Action Processor
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
try {
    // Action: Add Flashcard
    if ($action === 'add') {
        $front = trim((string) ($input['front'] ?? ''));
        $back  = trim((string) ($input['back'] ?? ''));

        if ($front === '' || $back === '') {
            respond(422, ['error' => 'Both the front and back of the card are required.']);
        }
        if (mb_strlen($front, 'UTF-8') > 5000 || mb_strlen($back, 'UTF-8') > 5000) {
            respond(422, ['error' => 'Each side must be 5000 characters or fewer.']);
        }

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO flashcards (deck_id, front, back) VALUES (?, ?, ?)");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "iss", $deck_id, $front, $back);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $newCardId = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        // Fetch newly created card record with defaults
        $fetchStmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "SELECT id, deck_id, front, back, interval_days, next_review_at, review_count, lapses, created_at, updated_at FROM flashcards WHERE id = ?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($fetchStmt, "i", $newCardId);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($fetchStmt);
        $card = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_stmt_get_result($fetchStmt));
        mysqli_stmt_close($fetchStmt);

        respond(200, [
            'ok'   => true,
            'card' => $card
        ]);
    }

    // Action: Update Flashcard
    if ($action === 'update') {
        $card_id        = filter_var($input['card_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
        $front          = trim((string) ($input['front'] ?? ''));
        $back           = trim((string) ($input['back'] ?? ''));
        $next_review_at = trim((string) ($input['next_review_at'] ?? ''));

        if (!$card_id || $card_id < 1) {
            respond(400, ['error' => 'Invalid card specified.']);
        }
        if ($front === '' || $back === '') {
            respond(422, ['error' => 'Both the front and back of the card are required.']);
        }
        if (mb_strlen($front, 'UTF-8') > 5000 || mb_strlen($back, 'UTF-8') > 5000) {
            respond(422, ['error' => 'Each side must be 5000 characters or fewer.']);
        }

        // Validate and sanitize custom next_review_at if provided
        $formattedNextReview = null;
        if ($next_review_at !== '') {
            $dt = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d\TH:i', $next_review_at) 
               ?: DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d H:i:s', $next_review_at);
            if ($dt !== false) {
                $formattedNextReview = $dt->format('Y-m-d H:i:s');
            }
        }

        if ($formattedNextReview !== null) {
            $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE flashcards SET front = ?, back = ?, next_review_at = ? WHERE id = ? AND deck_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL");
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "sssii", $front, $back, $formattedNextReview, $card_id, $deck_id);
        } else {
            $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE flashcards SET front = ?, back = ? WHERE id = ? AND deck_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL");
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ssii", $front, $back, $card_id, $deck_id);
        }

        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        // Fetch refreshed card record
        $fetchStmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "SELECT id, deck_id, front, back, interval_days, next_review_at, review_count, lapses, created_at, updated_at FROM flashcards WHERE id = ?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($fetchStmt, "i", $card_id);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($fetchStmt);
        $card = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_stmt_get_result($fetchStmt));
        mysqli_stmt_close($fetchStmt);

        respond(200, [
            'ok'   => true,
            'card' => $card
        ]);
    }

        // Actions: Archive / Restore / Permanent delete
    if (in_array($action, ['archive', 'restore', 'delete_permanent'], true)) {
        $card_id = filter_var($input['card_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
        if (!$card_id || $card_id < 1) {
            respond(400, ['error' => 'Invalid card specified.']);
        }

        if ($action === 'archive') {
            $ok = ArchiveService::archive($conn, 'flashcards', $card_id, $user_id);
        } elseif ($action === 'restore') {
            $ok = ArchiveService::restore($conn, 'flashcards', $card_id, $user_id);
        } else {
            $ok = ArchiveService::deletePermanently($conn, 'flashcards', $card_id, $user_id);
        }

        if (!$ok) {
            respond(404, ['error' => 'Card not found.']);
        }
        respond(200, ['ok' => true, 'card_id' => $card_id]);
    }

    // Action: Bulk Duplicate
    if ($action === 'bulk_duplicate') {
        $card_ids = $input['card_ids'] ?? [];
        if (!is_array($card_ids) || empty($card_ids)) respond(400, ['error' => 'No cards selected.']);
        $card_ids = array_filter(array_map('intval', $card_ids), fn($id) => $id > 0);
        if (count($card_ids) > 1000) respond(400, ['error' => 'Batch size limited to 1000.']);

        $newCards = FlashcardService::bulkDuplicate($conn, $deck_id, $card_ids, $user_id);
        respond(200, ['ok' => true, 'cards' => $newCards]);
    }

    // Action: Bulk Move
    if ($action === 'bulk_move') {
        $card_ids = $input['card_ids'] ?? [];
        $target_deck_id = filter_var($input['target_deck_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
        if (!$target_deck_id) respond(400, ['error' => 'Invalid target deck.']);
        if (!is_array($card_ids) || empty($card_ids)) respond(400, ['error' => 'No cards selected.']);
        $card_ids = array_filter(array_map('intval', $card_ids), fn($id) => $id > 0);
        if (count($card_ids) > 1000) respond(400, ['error' => 'Batch size limited to 1000.']);

        $affected = FlashcardService::bulkMove($conn, $deck_id, $target_deck_id, $card_ids, $user_id);
        respond(200, ['ok' => true, 'affected' => $affected]);
    }

    // Action: Bulk Reset
    if ($action === 'bulk_reset') {
        $card_ids = $input['card_ids'] ?? [];
        if (!is_array($card_ids) || empty($card_ids)) respond(400, ['error' => 'No cards selected.']);
        $card_ids = array_filter(array_map('intval', $card_ids), fn($id) => $id > 0);
        if (count($card_ids) > 1000) respond(400, ['error' => 'Batch size limited to 1000.']);

        $affected = FlashcardService::bulkReset($conn, $deck_id, $card_ids, $user_id);
        respond(200, ['ok' => true, 'affected' => $affected]);
    }

    // Action: Bulk Archive
    if ($action === 'bulk_archive') {
        $card_ids = $input['card_ids'] ?? [];
        if (!is_array($card_ids) || empty($card_ids)) respond(400, ['error' => 'No cards selected.']);
        $card_ids = array_filter(array_map('intval', $card_ids), fn($id) => $id > 0);
        if (count($card_ids) > 1000) respond(400, ['error' => 'Batch size limited to 1000.']);

        $archivedCount = 0;
        foreach ($card_ids as $id) {
            if (ArchiveService::archive($conn, 'flashcards', $id, $user_id)) {
                $archivedCount++;
            }
        }
        respond(200, ['ok' => true, 'affected' => $archivedCount]);
    }

        // Actions: Bulk Restore / Bulk Permanent Delete (archive view)
    if (in_array($action, ['bulk_restore', 'bulk_delete_permanent'], true)) {
        $card_ids = $input['card_ids'] ?? [];
        if (!is_array($card_ids) || empty($card_ids)) respond(400, ['error' => 'No cards selected.']);
        $card_ids = array_filter(array_map('intval', $card_ids), fn($id) => $id > 0);
        if (count($card_ids) > 1000) respond(400, ['error' => 'Batch size limited to 1000.']);

        $affected = 0;
        foreach ($card_ids as $id) {
            $ok = $action === 'bulk_restore'
                ? ArchiveService::restore($conn, 'flashcards', $id, $user_id)
                : ArchiveService::deletePermanently($conn, 'flashcards', $id, $user_id);
            if ($ok) $affected++;
        }
        respond(200, ['ok' => true, 'affected' => $affected]);
    }
    
    respond(400, ['error' => 'Unknown action.']);

} catch (Throwable $e) {
    error_log('flashcard-actions.php: ' . $e->getMessage());
    respond(500, ['error' => 'Something went wrong. Please try again.']);
}

FlashcardService.php
```
<?php
/**
 * FlashcardService
 * 
 * Centralized service layer for fetching deck details, managing flashcard 
 * operations, and enforcing data integrity and user authorization.
 */
require_once __DIR__ . '/ArchiveService.php';

class FlashcardService
{
    /**
     * Retrieve single deck details and verify user ownership.
     * 
     * @param mysqli $conn Active database connection instance.
     * @param int $deckId Target deck ID.
     * @param int $userId Authenticated user ID.
     * @return array|null Returns deck array if authorized and active, null otherwise.
     */
    public static function getDeckDetails(mysqli $conn, int $deckId, int $userId): ?array
    {
        $sql = "SELECT d.id, 
                       d.title, 
                       d.created_at,
                       (SELECT COUNT(*) 
                          FROM flashcards f 
                         WHERE f.deck_id = d.id AND f.deleted_at IS NULL) AS card_count,
                       (SELECT COUNT(*) 
                          FROM flashcards f 
                         WHERE f.deck_id = d.id AND f.deleted_at IS NULL AND f.next_review_at <= NOW()) AS due_count,
                       (SELECT COUNT(*)
                          FROM flashcards f
                         WHERE f.deck_id = d.id AND f.deleted_at IS NOT NULL) AS archived_count
                  FROM decks d
                 WHERE d.id = ? AND d.user_id = ? AND d.deleted_at IS NULL";

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql);
        if (!$stmt) {
            return null;
        }

        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ii", $deckId, $userId);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);
        $deck = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        return $deck ?: null;
    }

    /**
     * Fetch active flashcards belonging to a specific deck with optional search filtering.
     * 
     * @param mysqli $conn Active database connection instance.
     * @param int $deckId Target deck ID.
     * @param string $search Search query to match against front or back content.
     * @return array Array of flashcard records.
     */
        public static function getDeckFlashcards(mysqli $conn, int $deckId, string $search = '', bool $archived = false): array
    {
        $search      = trim($search);
        $deletedCond = $archived ? 'IS NOT NULL' : 'IS NULL';
        $orderBy     = $archived ? 'f.deleted_at DESC, f.id DESC' : 'f.id DESC';
        $retention   = ArchiveService::RETENTION_DAYS;

        $sql = "SELECT f.id, f.deck_id, f.front, f.back, f.interval_days, f.next_review_at,
                    f.review_count, f.lapses, f.created_at, f.updated_at, f.deleted_at,
                    GREATEST(0, ? - TIMESTAMPDIFF(DAY, f.deleted_at, NOW())) AS days_left,
                    (SELECT MAX(l.reviewed_at)
                        FROM review_logs l WHERE l.card_id = f.id) AS last_reviewed_at,
                    (SELECT ROUND(100 * SUM(l.rating = 'remembered') / COUNT(*))
                        FROM review_logs l WHERE l.card_id = f.id) AS accuracy,
                    (SELECT ROUND(AVG(l.response_time_ms))
                        FROM review_logs l WHERE l.card_id = f.id) AS avg_response_ms
                FROM flashcards f
                WHERE f.deck_id = ? AND f.deleted_at {$deletedCond}";

        if ($search !== '') {
            $sql .= " AND (f.front LIKE ? OR f.back LIKE ?)";
        }
        $sql .= " ORDER BY {$orderBy}";

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql);
        if (!$stmt) {
            return [];
        }

        if ($search !== '') {
            $like = '%' . $search . '%';
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "iiss", $retention, $deckId, $like, $like);
        } else {
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ii", $retention, $deckId);
        }

        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);

        $cards = [];
        while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
            $cards[] = [
                'id'               => (int) $row['id'],
                'deck_id'          => (int) $row['deck_id'],
                'front'            => $row['front'],
                'back'             => $row['back'],
                'interval_days'    => (int) $row['interval_days'],
                'next_review_at'   => $row['next_review_at'],
                'review_count'     => (int) $row['review_count'],
                'lapses'           => (int) $row['lapses'],
                'created_at'       => $row['created_at'],
                'updated_at'       => $row['updated_at'],
                'archived_at'      => $row['deleted_at'],
                'days_left'        => $row['days_left'] !== null ? (int) $row['days_left'] : null,
                'last_reviewed_at' => $row['last_reviewed_at'],
                'accuracy'         => $row['accuracy'] !== null ? (int) $row['accuracy'] : null,
                'avg_response_ms'  => $row['avg_response_ms'] !== null ? (int) $row['avg_response_ms'] : null,
            ];
        }
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        return $cards;
    }

    /**
     * Fetch a single flashcard record and verify deck ownership.
     * 
     * @param mysqli $conn Active database connection instance.
     * @param int $cardId Target flashcard ID.
     * @param int $userId Authenticated user ID.
     * @return array|null Returns flashcard array if found and authorized, null otherwise.
     */
    public static function getFlashcardById(mysqli $conn, int $cardId, int $userId): ?array
    {
        $sql = "SELECT f.id, f.deck_id, f.front, f.back, f.next_review_at, f.interval_days, f.review_count, f.lapses, f.created_at, f.updated_at
                  FROM flashcards f
                  JOIN decks d ON f.deck_id = d.id
                 WHERE f.id = ? AND d.user_id = ? AND f.deleted_at IS NULL AND d.deleted_at IS NULL";

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql);
        if (!$stmt) {
            return null;
        }

        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ii", $cardId, $userId);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);
        $card = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        return $card ?: null;
    }

    /**
     * Safely escape HTML string values for view rendering.
     * 
     * @param mixed $value Raw string content.
     * @return string Escaped HTML string.
     */
    public static function h($value): string
    {
        return htmlspecialchars((string) $value, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
    }

    /**
     * Fetch all active decks for a user (used for the Move dialog).
     */
    public static function getUserDecks(mysqli $conn, int $userId): array
    {
        $sql = "SELECT id, title FROM decks WHERE user_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY title ASC";
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql);
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $userId);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);
        $decks = [];
        while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
            $decks[] = ['id' => (int)$row['id'], 'title' => $row['title']];
        }
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
        return $decks;
    }

    /**
     * Bulk Duplicate: Copies front/back of specified cards into the same deck as new cards.
     */
    public static function bulkDuplicate(mysqli $conn, int $deckId, array $cardIds, int $userId): array
    {
        if (empty($cardIds)) return [];

        $placeholders = implode(',', array_fill(0, count($cardIds), '?'));
        $types = str_repeat('i', count($cardIds)) . 'ii';
        $params = array_merge($cardIds, [$deckId, $userId]);

        // Fetch original content first to ensure we only copy owned, non-deleted cards
        $fetchSql = "SELECT f.front, f.back FROM flashcards f 
                     JOIN decks d ON f.deck_id = d.id 
                     WHERE f.id IN ($placeholders) AND f.deck_id = ? AND d.user_id = ? AND f.deleted_at IS NULL";

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $fetchSql);
        $bindParams = [$types];
        foreach ($params as $k => $v) {
            $bindParams[] = &$params[$k];
        }
        call_user_func_array([$stmt, 'bind_param'], $bindParams);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);

        $cardsToInsert = [];
        while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
            $cardsToInsert[] = $row;
        }
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        if (empty($cardsToInsert)) return [];

        mysqli_begin_transaction($conn);
        $newIds = [];
        try {
            $insertStmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO flashcards (deck_id, front, back) VALUES (?, ?, ?)");
            foreach ($cardsToInsert as $card) {
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($insertStmt, "iss", $deckId, $card['front'], $card['back']);
                mysqli_stmt_execute($insertStmt);
                $newIds[] = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
            }
            mysqli_stmt_close($insertStmt);
            mysqli_commit($conn);
        } catch (Exception $e) {
            mysqli_rollback($conn);
            throw $e;
        }

        // Return full card objects for the UI
        $newPlaceholders = implode(',', array_fill(0, count($newIds), '?'));
        $newTypes = str_repeat('i', count($newIds));
        $newStmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "SELECT id, deck_id, front, back, interval_days, next_review_at, review_count, lapses, created_at, updated_at, deleted_at AS archived_at FROM flashcards WHERE id IN ($newPlaceholders) ORDER BY id DESC");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($newStmt, $newTypes, ...$newIds);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($newStmt);
        $newResult = mysqli_stmt_get_result($newStmt);

        $insertedCards = [];
        while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($newResult)) {
            $row['accuracy'] = null;
            $row['avg_response_ms'] = null;
            $row['last_reviewed_at'] = null;
            $row['days_left'] = null;
            $insertedCards[] = $row;
        }
        mysqli_stmt_close($newStmt);
        return $insertedCards;
    }

    /**
     * Bulk Move: Moves specified cards to another owned deck.
     */
    public static function bulkMove(mysqli $conn, int $sourceDeckId, int $targetDeckId, array $cardIds, int $userId): int
    {
        if (empty($cardIds) || $sourceDeckId === $targetDeckId) return 0;

        // Verify target deck ownership
        $checkStmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "SELECT id FROM decks WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($checkStmt, "ii", $targetDeckId, $userId);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($checkStmt);
        mysqli_stmt_store_result($checkStmt);
        $ownsTarget = mysqli_stmt_num_rows($checkStmt) === 1;
        mysqli_stmt_close($checkStmt);

        $placeholders = implode(',', array_fill(0, count($cardIds), '?'));
        $types = 'i' . str_repeat('i', count($cardIds)) . 'ii';
        $params = array_merge([$targetDeckId], $cardIds, [$sourceDeckId, $userId]);

        $sql = "UPDATE flashcards f 
                JOIN decks d ON f.deck_id = d.id 
                SET f.deck_id = ? 
                WHERE f.id IN ($placeholders) AND f.deck_id = ? AND d.user_id = ? AND f.deleted_at IS NULL";

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql);
        $bindParams = [$types];
        foreach ($params as $k => $v) {
            $bindParams[] = &$params[$k];
        }
        call_user_func_array([$stmt, 'bind_param'], $bindParams);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $affected = mysqli_stmt_affected_rows($stmt);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
        return $affected;
    }

    /**
     * Bulk Reset: Resets progress metrics for specified cards.
     */
    public static function bulkReset(mysqli $conn, int $deckId, array $cardIds, int $userId): int
    {
        if (empty($cardIds)) return 0;

        $placeholders = implode(',', array_fill(0, count($cardIds), '?'));
        $types = 'i' . str_repeat('i', count($cardIds)) . 'ii';
        $params = array_merge([$userId], $cardIds, [$deckId, $userId]);

        $sql = "UPDATE flashcards f 
                JOIN decks d ON f.deck_id = d.id 
                SET f.interval_days = COALESCE((SELECT initial_interval_days FROM user_settings WHERE user_id = ?), 1),
                    f.next_review_at = NOW(),
                    f.review_count = 0,
                    f.lapses = 0
                WHERE f.id IN ($placeholders) AND f.deck_id = ? AND d.user_id = ? AND f.deleted_at IS NULL";

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql);
        $bindParams = [$types];
        foreach ($params as $k => $v) {
            $bindParams[] = &$params[$k];
        }
        call_user_func_array([$stmt, 'bind_param'], $bindParams);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $affected = mysqli_stmt_affected_rows($stmt);
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

        return $affected;
    }
}
```

ReviewHubService.php
```
<?php
/**
 * ReviewHubService
 *
 * Read-only data + formatting helpers for the Review Hub page (review.php, no deck_id).
 * All "seconds" values are computed in SQL (TIMESTAMPDIFF) so PHP/MySQL timezone
 * differences cannot skew them.
 *
 * Place next to FlashcardService.php (the api/ folder).
 */
class ReviewHubService
{
    /** Cards scheduled within this many days count as "upcoming". */
    public const UPCOMING_DAYS = 2;

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Queries
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Every non-archived deck that has cards due now or coming up soon.
     *
     * @return array<int, array{
     *   id:int, title:string, totalCards:int, dueCount:int, upcomingCount:int,
     *   overdueSeconds:?int, nextDueSeconds:?int
     * }>
     */
    public static function getHubDecks(mysqli $conn, int $userId): array
    {
        $days = (int) self::UPCOMING_DAYS;

        $sql = "SELECT d.id, d.title,
                       COUNT(f.id) AS total_cards,
                       COALESCE(SUM(f.next_review_at <= NOW()), 0) AS due_count,
                       COALESCE(SUM(f.next_review_at > NOW()
                                    AND f.next_review_at <= DATE_ADD(NOW(), INTERVAL $days DAY)), 0) AS upcoming_count,
                       TIMESTAMPDIFF(SECOND,
                           MIN(CASE WHEN f.next_review_at <= NOW() THEN f.next_review_at END),
                           NOW()) AS overdue_seconds,
                       TIMESTAMPDIFF(SECOND, NOW(),
                           MIN(CASE WHEN f.next_review_at > NOW()
                                     AND f.next_review_at <= DATE_ADD(NOW(), INTERVAL $days DAY)
                                    THEN f.next_review_at END)) AS next_due_seconds
                FROM decks d
                LEFT JOIN flashcards f ON f.deck_id = d.id AND f.deleted_at IS NULL
                WHERE d.user_id = ? AND d.deleted_at IS NULL
                GROUP BY d.id, d.title
                HAVING due_count > 0 OR upcoming_count > 0";

        $decks = [];
        if ($stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql)) {
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $userId);
            mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
            $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);
            while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
                $decks[] = [
                    'id'             => (int) $row['id'],
                    'title'          => (string) $row['title'],
                    'totalCards'     => (int) $row['total_cards'],
                    'dueCount'       => (int) $row['due_count'],
                    'upcomingCount'  => (int) $row['upcoming_count'],
                    'overdueSeconds' => $row['overdue_seconds'] === null ? null : (int) $row['overdue_seconds'],
                    'nextDueSeconds' => $row['next_due_seconds'] === null ? null : (int) $row['next_due_seconds'],
                ];
            }
            mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
        }
        return $decks;
    }

    /**
     * Seconds until the user's next card becomes due (no 48h limit), or null
     * when there are no scheduled cards at all.
     */
    public static function getNextDueSeconds(mysqli $conn, int $userId): ?int
    {
        $sql = "SELECT TIMESTAMPDIFF(SECOND, NOW(), MIN(f.next_review_at))
                FROM flashcards f
                JOIN decks d ON d.id = f.deck_id
                WHERE d.user_id = ? AND d.deleted_at IS NULL
                  AND f.deleted_at IS NULL AND f.next_review_at > NOW()";

        $secs = null;
        if ($stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql)) {
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $userId);
            mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
            mysqli_stmt_bind_result($stmt, $secs);
            mysqli_stmt_fetch($stmt);
            mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
        }
        return $secs === null ? null : (int) $secs;
    }

    /**
     * Latest completed review sessions.
     *
     * @return array<int, array{
     *   deckId:int, deckTitle:string, cardsReviewed:int, rememberedCount:int,
     *   accuracy:int, durationSeconds:?int, formattedTime:string
     * }>
     */
    public static function getRecentSessions(mysqli $conn, int $userId, int $limit = 8): array
    {
        $limit = max(1, min($limit, 50));

        $sql = "SELECT rs.deck_id, d.title AS deck_title, rs.total_reviewed, rs.remembered_count,
                       rs.accuracy_percentage,
                       TIMESTAMPDIFF(SECOND, rs.started_at, rs.ended_at) AS duration_seconds,
                       TIMESTAMPDIFF(SECOND, rs.ended_at, NOW()) AS ended_ago_seconds
                FROM review_sessions rs
                JOIN decks d ON d.id = rs.deck_id
                WHERE rs.user_id = ? AND rs.ended_at IS NOT NULL
                ORDER BY rs.ended_at DESC, rs.id DESC
                LIMIT ?";

        $sessions = [];
        if ($stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql)) {
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ii", $userId, $limit);
            mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
            $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);
            while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
                $sessions[] = [
                    'deckId'          => (int) $row['deck_id'],
                    'deckTitle'       => (string) $row['deck_title'],
                    'cardsReviewed'   => (int) $row['total_reviewed'],
                    'rememberedCount' => (int) $row['remembered_count'],
                    'accuracy'        => (int) $row['accuracy_percentage'],
                    'durationSeconds' => $row['duration_seconds'] === null ? null : (int) $row['duration_seconds'],
                    'formattedTime'   => self::formatAgo((int) $row['ended_ago_seconds']),
                ];
            }
            mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
        }
        return $sessions;
    }

    /**
     * Cards reviewed today and the current study-day streak.
     * Same rules as the dashboard: a day counts when a completed session
     * reviewed at least one card; the streak is alive if the latest study day
     * is today or yesterday.
     *
     * @return array{reviewedToday:int, currentStreak:int}
     */
    public static function getStreakInfo(mysqli $conn, int $userId): array
    {
        $sql = "SELECT DATE(started_at) AS study_date, SUM(total_reviewed) AS reviewed
                FROM review_sessions
                WHERE user_id = ? AND ended_at IS NOT NULL
                GROUP BY DATE(started_at)
                HAVING SUM(total_reviewed) > 0
                ORDER BY study_date DESC";

        $days = [];
        if ($stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql)) {
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $userId);
            mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
            $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);
            while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
                $days[$row['study_date']] = (int) $row['reviewed'];
            }
            mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
        }

        $today     = date('Y-m-d');
        $yesterday = date('Y-m-d', strtotime('-1 day'));

        $streak = 0;
        if (isset($days[$today]) || isset($days[$yesterday])) {
            $cursor = new DateTime(isset($days[$today]) ? $today : $yesterday);
            while (isset($days[$cursor->format('Y-m-d')])) {
                $streak++;
                $cursor->modify('-1 day');
            }
        }

        return [
            'reviewedToday' => $days[$today] ?? 0,
            'currentStreak' => $streak,
        ];
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Table helpers (pure PHP, no DB)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** Decks with cards due, most due first (oldest overdue breaks ties). */
    public static function dueDecks(array $hubDecks): array
    {
        $due = array_values(array_filter($hubDecks, fn($d) => $d['dueCount'] > 0));
        usort($due, function ($a, $b) {
            return [$b['dueCount'], $b['overdueSeconds'] ?? 0]
               <=> [$a['dueCount'], $a['overdueSeconds'] ?? 0];
        });
        return $due;
    }

    /** Decks with cards coming up soon, soonest first. */
    public static function upcomingDecks(array $hubDecks): array
    {
        $up = array_values(array_filter($hubDecks, fn($d) => $d['upcomingCount'] > 0));
        usort($up, fn($a, $b) => ($a['nextDueSeconds'] ?? PHP_INT_MAX) <=> ($b['nextDueSeconds'] ?? PHP_INT_MAX));
        return $up;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Formatting
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** "5 min", "3 h", "2 d" (never shows 0). */
    public static function formatSpan(int $seconds): string
    {
        $seconds = max(0, $seconds);
        if ($seconds < 3600)  return max(1, (int) floor($seconds / 60)) . ' min';
        if ($seconds < 86400) return (int) floor($seconds / 3600) . ' h';
        return (int) floor($seconds / 86400) . ' d';
    }

    /** "just now", "5 mins ago", "1 hour ago", "3 days ago". */
    public static function formatAgo(int $seconds): string
    {
        $seconds = max(0, $seconds);
        if ($seconds < 60) return 'just now';

        if ($seconds < 3600) {
            $n = (int) floor($seconds / 60);
            return $n . ($n === 1 ? ' min ago' : ' mins ago');
        }
        if ($seconds < 86400) {
            $n = (int) floor($seconds / 3600);
            return $n . ($n === 1 ? ' hour ago' : ' hours ago');
        }
        $n = (int) floor($seconds / 86400);
        return $n . ($n === 1 ? ' day ago' : ' days ago');
    }

    /** "45s", "4m 12s", "1h 5m", or an em dash when unknown. */
    public static function formatDuration(?int $seconds): string
    {
        if ($seconds === null || $seconds <= 0) return '—';
        if ($seconds < 60) return $seconds . 's';
        if ($seconds < 3600) {
            $m = (int) floor($seconds / 60);
            $s = $seconds % 60;
            return $s > 0 ? "{$m}m {$s}s" : "{$m}m";
        }
        $h = (int) floor($seconds / 3600);
        $m = (int) floor(($seconds % 3600) / 60);
        return $m > 0 ? "{$h}h {$m}m" : "{$h}h";
    }

    /** Tailwind classes for an accuracy badge (matches the deck grid thresholds). */
    public static function accuracyBadgeClass(int $accuracy): string
    {
        if ($accuracy >= 80) return 'text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200/80';
        if ($accuracy >= 50) return 'text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 border-amber-200/80';
        return 'text-rose-700 bg-rose-50 border-rose-200/80';
    }

    /** Shorthand for htmlspecialchars with the project's flags. */
    public static function h($value): string
    {
        return htmlspecialchars((string) $value, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
    }
}
```

save-session.php
```
<?php
session_start();
header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8');
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] !== 'POST') {
    http_response_code(405);
    echo json_encode(['error' => 'Method not allowed.']);
    exit;
}

if (!isset($_SESSION['logged_in']) || $_SESSION['logged_in'] !== true || !isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(['error' => 'Unauthorized']);
    exit;
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/db.php';

$userId = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

$input = json_decode(file_get_contents('php://input'), true);
if (!is_array($input)) {
    http_response_code(400);
    echo json_encode(['error' => 'Invalid request.']);
    exit;
}

// Anti-CSRF: same session token the card endpoints use
$token = $input['csrf_token'] ?? '';
if (empty($_SESSION['csrf_token']) || !is_string($token) || !hash_equals($_SESSION['csrf_token'], $token)) {
    http_response_code(403);
    echo json_encode(['error' => 'Security check failed. Refresh the page and try again.']);
    exit;
}

$deckId  = filter_var($input['deck_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
$results = $input['results'] ?? null;

$durationSeconds = filter_var($input['duration_seconds'] ?? 0, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
$durationSeconds = ($durationSeconds === false) ? 0 : max(0, min($durationSeconds, 6 * 3600));

if (!$deckId || $deckId < 1) {
    http_response_code(400);
    echo json_encode(['error' => 'Invalid deck.']);
    exit;
}
if (!is_array($results) || empty($results)) {
    echo json_encode(['ok' => true, 'saved' => 0, 'msg' => 'No data to save']);
    exit;
}
if (count($results) > 1000) {
    http_response_code(400);
    echo json_encode(['error' => 'Batch size limited to 1000.']);
    exit;
}

// Verify the deck belongs to this user (and is not archived)
$ownStmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "SELECT id FROM decks WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL");
mysqli_stmt_bind_param($ownStmt, "ii", $deckId, $userId);
mysqli_stmt_execute($ownStmt);
mysqli_stmt_store_result($ownStmt);
$ownsDeck = mysqli_stmt_num_rows($ownStmt) === 1;
mysqli_stmt_close($ownStmt);

if (!$ownsDeck) {
    http_response_code(404);
    echo json_encode(['error' => 'Deck not found or access denied.']);
    exit;
}

mysqli_begin_transaction($conn);

try {
    // NOTE: single-table SET assignments run left to right, so next_review_at
    // must come BEFORE interval_days to see the old interval. Keep this order,
    // and do not turn these into JOIN updates (order is not guaranteed there).
    $stmtRemember = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE flashcards
        SET review_count = review_count + 1,
            next_review_at = DATE_ADD(NOW(), INTERVAL GREATEST(1, interval_days * 2) DAY),
            interval_days = GREATEST(1, interval_days * 2),
            updated_at = NOW()
        WHERE id = ? AND deck_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL AND next_review_at <= NOW()");

    $stmtForgot = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE flashcards
        SET review_count = review_count + 1,
            lapses = lapses + 1,
            interval_days = 0,
            next_review_at = NOW(),
            updated_at = NOW()
        WHERE id = ? AND deck_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL AND next_review_at <= NOW()");

    if (!$stmtRemember || !$stmtForgot) {
        throw new RuntimeException('Could not prepare statements.');
    }

    $saved = 0;
    $rememberedCount = 0;

    foreach ($results as $cardId => $status) {
        $cid = filter_var($cardId, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
        if (!$cid || $cid < 1) continue;

        if ($status === 'remembered')    $stmt = $stmtRemember;
        elseif ($status === 'forgotten') $stmt = $stmtForgot;
        else continue;

        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ii", $cid, $deckId);
        if (!mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
            throw new RuntimeException('Card update failed.');
        }
        if (mysqli_stmt_affected_rows($stmt) > 0) {   // only count cards actually updated
            $saved++;
            if ($status === 'remembered') $rememberedCount++;
        }
    }

    mysqli_stmt_close($stmtRemember);
    mysqli_stmt_close($stmtForgot);

    if ($saved === 0) {
        mysqli_rollback($conn);
        echo json_encode(['ok' => true, 'saved' => 0, 'msg' => 'Nothing to save']);
        exit;
    }

    // Session log for dashboard analytics
    $accuracy = (int) round(($rememberedCount / $saved) * 100);

    $logStmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO review_sessions
        (user_id, deck_id, total_reviewed, remembered_count, accuracy_percentage, started_at, ended_at)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL ? SECOND), NOW())");
    if (!$logStmt) {
        throw new RuntimeException('Could not prepare session log.');
    }
    mysqli_stmt_bind_param($logStmt, "iiiiii", $userId, $deckId, $saved, $rememberedCount, $accuracy, $durationSeconds);
    if (!mysqli_stmt_execute($logStmt)) {
        throw new RuntimeException('Session log failed.');
    }
    mysqli_stmt_close($logStmt);

    mysqli_commit($conn);
    echo json_encode(['ok' => true, 'saved' => $saved]);

} catch (Throwable $e) {
    mysqli_rollback($conn);
    error_log('save-session.php: ' . $e->getMessage());
    http_response_code(500);
    echo json_encode(['error' => 'Database exception occurred']);
}
```
